========== tickets/models.py ==========

from django.conf import settings
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db import models
from django.utils.text import slugify



class Category(models.Model):
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100, unique=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name_plural = "categories"
        ordering = ["name"]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name


class Tag(models.Model):
    """Admin-managed tags (optional) jo tickets par lagaye ja sakte hain."""
    name = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["name"]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name


class Priority(models.Model):
    """Admin-defined priority levels (default: Low, Medium, High, Critical)."""
    name = models.CharField(max_length=50, unique=True)
    slug = models.SlugField(max_length=50, unique=True, editable=False)
    level = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(
        default=1, help_text="Higher number = more urgent."
    )
    is_active = models.BooleanField(
        default=True, help_text="Inactive priorities cannot be used on new tickets."
    )

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["level", "id"]
        verbose_name_plural = "priorities"

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        # slug sirf pehli baar bante waqt banta hai, tickets isi ko store karte hain
        if not self.slug:
            self.slug = slugify(self.name).replace("-", "_")
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name


def validate_priority(value):
    """Ticket ki priority kisi active Priority ka slug honi chahiye."""
    if not Priority.objects.filter(slug=value, is_active=True).exists():
        raise ValidationError(f"'{value}' is not a valid priority.")


class Ticket(models.Model):
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        NEW = "new", "New"
        IN_PROGRESS = "in_progress", "In Progress"
        RESOLVED = "resolved", "Resolved"
        CLOSED = "closed", "Closed"

    title = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    description = models.TextField()
    category = models.ForeignKey(
        Category, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True,
        related_name="tickets",
    )
    priority = models.CharField(
        max_length=50, default="medium", validators=[validate_priority]
    )
    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.NEW
    )
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="created_tickets",
    )
    assigned_to = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True, related_name="assigned_tickets",
        limit_choices_to={"role": "support"},
    )
    tags = models.ManyToManyField(Tag, blank=True, related_name="tickets")
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
    resolved_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-created_at"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"#{self.pk} {self.title}"


class Comment(models.Model):
    ticket = models.ForeignKey(
        Ticket, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="comments"
    )
    author = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE
    )
    body = models.TextField()
    is_internal = models.BooleanField(
        default=False, help_text="Internal note: only staff can see it."
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["created_at"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"Comment by {self.author} on #{self.ticket_id}"


class Attachment(models.Model):
    ticket = models.ForeignKey(
        Ticket, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="attachments"
    )
    file = models.FileField(upload_to="attachments/%Y/%m/")
    uploaded_by = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.CASCADE
    )
    uploaded_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    def __str__(self):
        return self.file.name


class TicketHistory(models.Model):
    """Log of status/priority/assignment changes, so users can see a ticket's history."""
    ticket = models.ForeignKey(
        Ticket, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="history"
    )
    changed_by = models.ForeignKey(
        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True, blank=True,
    )
    field = models.CharField(max_length=30)
    old_value = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    new_value = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["created_at"]
        verbose_name_plural = "ticket history"

    def __str__(self):
        return f"#{self.ticket_id}: {self.field} {self.old_value} -> {self.new_value}"

from .site_settings import SystemSettings  #noqa: F401


========== tickets/notifications.py ==========

"""Email notifications (spec 3.9). Development me email terminal me print hota hai."""
import logging

from django.conf import settings
from django.core.mail import send_mail


log = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def notifications_enabled():
    """Admin settings (database) me notifications_enabled False ho to sab emails band."""
    from .site_settings import SystemSettings
    return SystemSettings.load().notifications_enabled


def _name(user):
    return user.get_full_name() or user.username


def _status_label(value):
    from .models import Ticket
    try:
        return Ticket.Status(value).label
    except ValueError:
        return value


def _send(user, subject, message):
    if not notifications_enabled():
        return
    if user is None or not user.is_active or not user.email:
        return
    try:
        send_mail(
            subject=subject,
            message=message,
            from_email=None,
            recipient_list=[user.email],
        )
    except Exception:
        # Email fail ho to bhi ticket ka kaam na ruke
        log.exception('Could not send notification email to %s', user.email)


def notify_ticket_created(ticket):
    """Customer ko confirmation: ticket ban gayi."""
    _send(
        ticket.created_by,
        f'[Ticket #{ticket.pk}] We received your request: {ticket.title}',
        f'Hello {_name(ticket.created_by)},\n\n'
        f'Your ticket has been created.\n\n'
        f'Ticket:   #{ticket.pk} {ticket.title}\n'
        f'Priority: {ticket.priority}\n'
        f'Status:   {_status_label(ticket.status)}\n\n'
        f'Our support team will get back to you soon.',
    )


def notify_status_changed(ticket, old_status):
    """Ticket ke owner (customer) ko: status badal gaya."""
    _send(
        ticket.created_by,
        f'[Ticket #{ticket.pk}] Status changed: '
        f'{_status_label(old_status)} -> {_status_label(ticket.status)}',
        f'Hello {_name(ticket.created_by)},\n\n'
        f'The status of your ticket has changed.\n\n'
        f'Ticket: #{ticket.pk} {ticket.title}\n'
        f'Status: {_status_label(old_status)} -> {_status_label(ticket.status)}',
    )


def notify_assigned(ticket):
    """Support staff ko: nayi ticket assign hui."""
    staff = ticket.assigned_to
    _send(
        staff,
        f'[Ticket #{ticket.pk}] Assigned to you: {ticket.title}',
        f'Hello {_name(staff)},\n\n'
        f'A ticket has been assigned to you.\n\n'
        f'Ticket:   #{ticket.pk} {ticket.title}\n'
        f'Customer: {ticket.created_by.username}\n'
        f'Priority: {ticket.priority}\n'
        f'Status:   {_status_label(ticket.status)}',
    )


========== tickets/signals.py ==========

"""
Ticket save hone par emails (API, admin panel, kahin se bhi badlo - sab jagah chalta hai):
  - nayi ticket        -> customer ko confirmation (aur assigned ho to staff ko bhi)
  - status badla       -> customer ko
  - staff assign hua   -> us support staff ko
"""
from django.db import transaction
from django.db.models.signals import post_save, pre_save
from django.dispatch import receiver

from .models import Ticket
from .notifications import (
    notify_assigned, notify_status_changed, notify_ticket_created,
)


@receiver(pre_save, sender=Ticket)
def remember_old_values(sender, instance, **kwargs):
    instance._old_status = None
    instance._old_assigned_to_id = None
    if instance.pk:
        old = (
            Ticket.objects.filter(pk=instance.pk)
            .values('status', 'assigned_to_id').first()
        )
        if old:
            instance._old_status = old['status']
            instance._old_assigned_to_id = old['assigned_to_id']


@receiver(post_save, sender=Ticket)
def send_ticket_notifications(sender, instance, created, **kwargs):
    ticket = instance

    if created:
        def after_create():
            notify_ticket_created(ticket)
            if ticket.assigned_to_id:
                notify_assigned(ticket)
        transaction.on_commit(after_create)
        return

    old_status = getattr(ticket, '_old_status', None)
    old_assigned = getattr(ticket, '_old_assigned_to_id', None)

    status_changed = old_status is not None and old_status != ticket.status
    newly_assigned = (
        ticket.assigned_to_id is not None and ticket.assigned_to_id != old_assigned
    )

    if status_changed or newly_assigned:
        def after_update():
            if status_changed:
                notify_status_changed(ticket, old_status)
            if newly_assigned:
                notify_assigned(ticket)
        transaction.on_commit(after_update)


========== tickets/site_settings.py ==========

"""Admin settings (spec 3.10): ek hi row wala SystemSettings model."""
from django.db import models


class SystemSettings(models.Model):
    allow_public_registration = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    max_upload_size_mb = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=5)
    max_files_per_request = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=5)
    allowed_file_types = models.CharField(
        max_length=200,
        default='gif,jpeg,jpg,pdf,png',
        help_text='Comma se alag extensions, jaise: jpg,png,pdf',
    )
    notifications_enabled = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = 'System settings'
        verbose_name_plural = 'System settings'

    def __str__(self):
        return 'System settings'

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        # Hamesha sirf ek row (id=1)
        self.pk = 1
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def delete(self, *args, **kwargs):
        # Settings row delete nahi hone dena
        pass

    @classmethod
    def load(cls):
        obj, _ = cls.objects.get_or_create(pk=1)
        return obj

    def allowed_types_list(self):
        return [
            t.strip().lower().lstrip('.')
            for t in self.allowed_file_types.split(',')
            if t.strip()
        ]


========== tickets/tests.py ==========

import shutil
import tempfile

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.core.files.uploadedfile import SimpleUploadedFile
from django.test import TestCase, override_settings
from rest_framework.authtoken.models import Token
from rest_framework.test import APIClient

from tickets.models import Priority, Tag, Ticket
from tickets.site_settings import SystemSettings

User = get_user_model()


class BaseAPITest(TestCase):
    """Common setup: users of each role, a priority, a tag, and a temp media folder."""

    def setUp(self):
        media = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        self.enterContext(override_settings(MEDIA_ROOT=media))
        self.addCleanup(shutil.rmtree, media, ignore_errors=True)

        self.admin = User.objects.create_user('admin1', 'a@x.com', 'pass12345', role='admin')
        self.support = User.objects.create_user('support1', 's@x.com', 'pass12345', role='support')
        self.customer = User.objects.create_user('cust1', 'c@x.com', 'pass12345', role='customer')
        self.customer2 = User.objects.create_user('cust2', 'c2@x.com', 'pass12345', role='customer')

        Priority.objects.get_or_create(name='Medium', defaults={'level': 2})
        self.tag = Tag.objects.create(name='bug')

    def client_for(self, user):
        client = APIClient()
        token, _ = Token.objects.get_or_create(user=user)
        client.credentials(HTTP_AUTHORIZATION='Token ' + token.key)
        return client

    def make_ticket(self, user, **extra):
        data = {'title': 'Printer broken', 'description': 'It will not print.', 'priority': 'medium'}
        data.update(extra)
        return self.client_for(user).post('/api/my-tickets/', data, format='json')


class AuthTests(BaseAPITest):
    def test_login_returns_token(self):
        res = APIClient().post('/api/login/', {'username': 'cust1', 'password': 'pass12345'}, format='json')
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200)
        self.assertIn('token', res.data)

    def test_login_wrong_password_is_rejected(self):
        res = APIClient().post('/api/login/', {'username': 'cust1', 'password': 'wrong'}, format='json')
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 401)

    def test_login_requires_both_fields(self):
        res = APIClient().post('/api/login/', {'username': 'cust1'}, format='json')
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 400)

    def test_token_gives_access_and_anonymous_does_not(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.client_for(self.customer).get('/api/my-tickets/').status_code, 200)
        self.assertIn(APIClient().get('/api/my-tickets/').status_code, (401, 403))


class TicketTests(BaseAPITest):
    def test_customer_creates_ticket_with_tag(self):
        res = self.make_ticket(self.customer, tags=[self.tag.id])
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 201)
        self.assertEqual(res.data['status'], 'new')
        self.assertEqual(res.data['tag_names'], ['bug'])
        self.assertEqual(Ticket.objects.get(pk=res.data['id']).created_by, self.customer)

    def test_invalid_priority_is_rejected(self):
        res = self.make_ticket(self.customer, priority='does_not_exist')
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 400)

    def test_customer_cannot_see_another_customers_ticket(self):
        ticket_id = self.make_ticket(self.customer).data['id']
        other = self.client_for(self.customer2)
        self.assertEqual(other.get(f'/api/my-tickets/{ticket_id}/').status_code, 404)
        self.assertEqual(other.get('/api/tickets/').data['count'], 0)

    def test_admin_sees_all_tickets(self):
        self.make_ticket(self.customer)
        self.make_ticket(self.customer2)
        res = self.client_for(self.admin).get('/api/tickets/')
        self.assertEqual(res.data['count'], 2)

    def test_search_by_keyword(self):
        self.make_ticket(self.customer, title='VPN is down')
        self.make_ticket(self.customer, title='Invoice question')
        res = self.client_for(self.customer).get('/api/tickets/?search=vpn')
        self.assertEqual(res.data['count'], 1)

    def test_filter_by_tag(self):
        self.make_ticket(self.customer, tags=[self.tag.id])
        self.make_ticket(self.customer)
        client = self.client_for(self.customer)
        self.assertEqual(client.get('/api/tickets/?tag=bug').data['count'], 1)
        self.assertEqual(client.get('/api/tickets/?tag=nothing').data['count'], 0)

    def test_customer_can_edit_only_while_new(self):
        ticket_id = self.make_ticket(self.customer).data['id']
        Ticket.objects.filter(pk=ticket_id).update(status='in_progress')
        res = self.client_for(self.customer).patch(
            f'/api/my-tickets/{ticket_id}/', {'title': 'Changed'}, format='json')
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 403)


class TagTests(BaseAPITest):
    def test_admin_can_create_tag(self):
        res = self.client_for(self.admin).post('/api/admin/tags/', {'name': 'urgent'}, format='json')
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 201)

    def test_customer_cannot_create_tag(self):
        res = self.client_for(self.customer).post('/api/admin/tags/', {'name': 'urgent'}, format='json')
        self.assertIn(res.status_code, (401, 403))

    def test_duplicate_tag_is_rejected(self):
        res = self.client_for(self.admin).post('/api/admin/tags/', {'name': 'bug'}, format='json')
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 400)


class AttachmentTests(BaseAPITest):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.ticket_id = self.make_ticket(self.customer).data['id']
        self.url = f'/api/tickets/{self.ticket_id}/attachments/'

    def upload(self, *files, user=None):
        client = self.client_for(user or self.customer)
        return client.post(self.url, {'files': list(files)}, format='multipart')

    def test_valid_upload(self):
        res = self.upload(SimpleUploadedFile('shot.png', b'x' * 100, content_type='image/png'))
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 201)
        self.assertEqual(len(res.data), 1)

    def test_disallowed_file_type_is_rejected(self):
        res = self.upload(SimpleUploadedFile('virus.exe', b'x' * 10))
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 400)

    def test_too_many_files_are_rejected(self):
        cfg = SystemSettings.load()
        cfg.max_files_per_request = 1
        cfg.save()
        res = self.upload(
            SimpleUploadedFile('a.png', b'x'), SimpleUploadedFile('b.png', b'x'))
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 400)

    def test_file_over_size_limit_is_rejected(self):
        cfg = SystemSettings.load()
        cfg.max_upload_size_mb = 1
        cfg.save()
        res = self.upload(SimpleUploadedFile('big.png', b'x' * (2 * 1024 * 1024)))
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 400)

    def test_other_customer_cannot_upload_to_ticket(self):
        res = self.upload(SimpleUploadedFile('a.png', b'x'), user=self.customer2)
        self.assertIn(res.status_code, (403, 404))


class AdminSettingsTests(BaseAPITest):
    def test_customer_cannot_open_settings(self):
        res = self.client_for(self.customer).get('/api/admin/settings/')
        self.assertIn(res.status_code, (401, 403))

    def test_admin_can_update_settings(self):
        res = self.client_for(self.admin).patch(
            '/api/admin/settings/', {'max_upload_size_mb': 3}, format='json')
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(SystemSettings.load().max_upload_size_mb, 3)

    def test_settings_validation(self):
        res = self.client_for(self.admin).patch(
            '/api/admin/settings/', {'max_upload_size_mb': 999}, format='json')
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 400)

    def test_public_registration_can_be_switched_off(self):
        self.client_for(self.admin).patch(
            '/api/admin/settings/', {'allow_public_registration': False}, format='json')
        res = APIClient().post(
            '/api/register/',
            {'username': 'newuser', 'email': 'n@x.com', 'password': 'pass12345'},
            format='json')
        self.assertEqual(res.status_code, 403)


class WebUITests(BaseAPITest):
    def test_ui_pages_render(self):
        client = APIClient()
        for url in ('/', '/login/', '/dashboard/', '/tickets/', '/tickets/new/', '/tickets/1/'):
            self.assertEqual(client.get(url).status_code, 200, url)

========== accounts/admin.py ==========

from django.contrib import admin
from django.contrib.auth.admin import UserAdmin

from .models import User


@admin.register(User)
class CustomUserAdmin(UserAdmin):
    list_display = ("username", "email", "role", "is_staff")
    list_filter = ("role", "is_staff", "is_active")
    fieldsets = UserAdmin.fieldsets + (("Helpdesk", {"fields": ("role",)}),)
    add_fieldsets = UserAdmin.add_fieldsets + (
        ("Helpdesk", {"fields": ("role",)}),
    )


========== accounts/api_docs.py ==========

"""
Swagger / OpenAPI documentation ke annotations.

Views ke code ko chhuye bina, yahan se har endpoint ko tag, description,
request body aur filters ka documentation diya jata hai.
accounts/urls.py is file ko import karti hai.
"""
import importlib
import logging

from drf_spectacular.types import OpenApiTypes
from drf_spectacular.utils import (
    OpenApiParameter,
    extend_schema,
    extend_schema_view,
    inline_serializer,
)
from rest_framework import serializers

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _apply(module_path, class_name, decorator):
    """View class par decorator lagao. Class na mile to skip (docs API ko na todein)."""
    try:
        module = importlib.import_module(module_path)
        view = getattr(module, class_name)
    except (ImportError, AttributeError) as exc:
        log.warning('API docs skipped for %s.%s (%s)', module_path, class_name, exc)
        return None
    decorator(view)
    return getattr(module, class_name)


def _serializer(module_path, class_name):
    try:
        return getattr(importlib.import_module(module_path), class_name)
    except (ImportError, AttributeError):
        return OpenApiTypes.OBJECT


V = 'accounts.views.'

# --------------------------------------------------------------- common
TICKET_FILTERS = [
    OpenApiParameter(
        'search', str,
        description='Ticket ID (12 ya #12), title, description ya comment ka keyword.',
    ),
    OpenApiParameter(
        'status', str,
        description='Comma se multiple: new, in_progress, resolved, closed.',
    ),
    OpenApiParameter(
        'priority', str,
        description='Comma se multiple priority slugs (low, medium, high, critical, ...).',
    ),
    OpenApiParameter(
        'category', str, description="Category id(s) comma se, ya 'none' (bina category)."
    ),
    OpenApiParameter(
        'created_from', OpenApiTypes.DATE, description='YYYY-MM-DD (is din ko mila kar).'
    ),
    OpenApiParameter(
        'created_to', OpenApiTypes.DATE, description='YYYY-MM-DD (is din ko mila kar).'
    ),
    OpenApiParameter(
        'ordering', str,
        enum=['-created_at', 'created_at', '-updated_at', 'updated_at',
              '-id', 'id', '-status', 'status'],
        description='Default: -created_at.',
    ),
]
ADMIN_ASSIGNED_FILTER = OpenApiParameter(
    'assigned_to', str,
    description="(Sirf Admin) staff user id ya 'unassigned'.",
)

MESSAGE_RESPONSE = {200: OpenApiTypes.OBJECT}


def tag(name):
    return extend_schema(tags=[name])


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ Auth
_apply(V + 'login_view', 'LoginView', extend_schema_view(
    get=extend_schema(
        tags=['Auth'], summary='Login endpoint info', auth=[],
        responses=MESSAGE_RESPONSE,
    ),
    post=extend_schema(
        tags=['Auth'], summary='Login (token lo)', auth=[],
        description='Username aur password do, response me `token` milta hai. '
                    'Baaki requests me header lagao: `Authorization: Token <token>`.',
        request=inline_serializer('LoginRequest', {
            'username': serializers.CharField(),
            'password': serializers.CharField(),
        }),
        responses=MESSAGE_RESPONSE,
    ),
))

_apply(V + 'register_view', 'RegisterView', extend_schema(
    tags=['Auth'], summary='Register (hamesha Customer role)', auth=[],
    description='Naya account hamesha `customer` role ke saath banta hai. '
                'Admin ne public registration band kiya ho to 403 aata hai.',
    request=_serializer(V + 'register_view', 'RegisterSerializer'),
    responses={201: OpenApiTypes.OBJECT},
))

_apply(V + 'logout_view', 'LogoutView', extend_schema(
    tags=['Auth'], summary='Logout (token delete)', request=None,
    responses=MESSAGE_RESPONSE,
))

_apply(V + 'forgot_reset_view', 'ForgotPasswordView', extend_schema(
    tags=['Auth'], summary='Password reset email bhejo', auth=[],
    request=inline_serializer('ForgotPasswordRequest', {
        'email': serializers.EmailField(),
    }),
    responses=MESSAGE_RESPONSE,
))
_apply(V + 'forgot_reset_view', 'ResetPasswordView', extend_schema(
    tags=['Auth'], summary='Email wale uid + token se naya password set karo', auth=[],
    request=inline_serializer('ResetPasswordRequest', {
        'uid': serializers.CharField(),
        'token': serializers.CharField(),
        'new_password': serializers.CharField(),
    }),
    responses=MESSAGE_RESPONSE,
))
_apply(V + 'change_password_view', 'ChangePasswordView', extend_schema(
    tags=['Auth'], summary='Password badlo (logged-in user)',
    request=inline_serializer('ChangePasswordRequest', {
        'old_password': serializers.CharField(),
        'new_password': serializers.CharField(),
    }),
    responses=MESSAGE_RESPONSE,
))
_apply(V + 'user_profile_view', 'UserProfileView', tag('Auth'))

# ----------------------------------------------------------- Customer
_apply(V + 'my_tickets_view', 'MyTicketsView', extend_schema(
    tags=['Customer tickets'],
    description='GET: meri tickets (filters: status, priority, search). POST: nayi ticket.',
))
_apply(V + 'my_tickets_view', 'MyTicketDetailView', tag('Customer tickets'))
_apply(V + 'ticket_history_view', 'MyTicketHistoryView', tag('Customer tickets'))

# --------------------------------------------- Tickets (all roles) / search
_apply(V + 'ticket_search_view', 'TicketSearchView', extend_schema(
    tags=['Tickets'], summary='Search & filter tickets (role ke hisaab se)',
    description='Admin ko sab tickets, Support Staff ko assigned tickets, '
                'Customer ko apni tickets milti hain. Result pages me aata hai.',
    parameters=TICKET_FILTERS + [ADMIN_ASSIGNED_FILTER],
))

_comment_serializer = _serializer(V + 'comment_views', 'CommentSerializer')
_apply(V + 'comment_views', 'TicketCommentView', extend_schema_view(
    get=extend_schema(
        tags=['Comments & attachments'], summary='Ticket ka comment thread',
        description='Customer ko internal notes nahi dikhte.',
        responses=_comment_serializer(many=True)
        if _comment_serializer is not OpenApiTypes.OBJECT else OpenApiTypes.OBJECT,
    ),
    post=extend_schema(
        tags=['Comments & attachments'], summary='Comment likho',
        description='`is_internal=true` sirf Support Staff / Admin laga sakta hai.',
        request=_comment_serializer, responses={201: _comment_serializer},
    ),
))

_attachment_serializer = _serializer(V + 'attachment_views', 'AttachmentSerializer')
_apply(V + 'attachment_views', 'TicketAttachmentView', extend_schema_view(
    get=extend_schema(
        tags=['Comments & attachments'], summary='Ticket ki files ki list',
        responses=_attachment_serializer(many=True)
        if _attachment_serializer is not OpenApiTypes.OBJECT else OpenApiTypes.OBJECT,
    ),
    post=extend_schema(
        tags=['Comments & attachments'], summary='Ek ya zyada files upload karo',
        description='Field naam `files` (repeat kar sakte ho). Allowed: jpg, jpeg, png, '
                    'gif, pdf. Har file max 5 MB, ek baar me max 5 files.',
        request={'multipart/form-data': inline_serializer('AttachmentUploadRequest', {
            'files': serializers.ListField(child=serializers.FileField()),
        })},
        responses={201: _attachment_serializer(many=True)
                   if _attachment_serializer is not OpenApiTypes.OBJECT
                   else OpenApiTypes.OBJECT},
    ),
))

# ------------------------------------------------------------ Support
_apply(V + 'support_views', 'SupportTicketListView', extend_schema(
    tags=['Support staff'], summary='Mujhe assigned tickets',
    parameters=TICKET_FILTERS[:4],
))

    tags=['Support staff'],
    description='PATCH: sirf `status` aur `priority`. Flow: New -> In Progress -> '
                'Resolved -> Closed.',

# -------------------------------------------------------------- Admin
for _cls in ('AdminUserListCreateView', 'AdminUserDetailView',
             'AdminCategoryListCreateView', 'AdminCategoryDetailView'):

    parameters=[
        OpenApiParameter('role', str, enum=['admin', 'support', 'customer']),

))

    tags=['Admin: tickets'], summary='Ticket manually assign karo',
    description='`{"assigned_to": <support user id>}` ya `null` (unassign).',

========== accounts/models.py ==========

from django.contrib.auth.models import AbstractUser
from django.db import models


class User(AbstractUser):
    """Custom user with a role: customer / agent / admin."""

    class Role(models.TextChoices):
        CUSTOMER = "customer", "Customer"
        SUPPORT = "support", "Support Staff"
        ADMIN = "admin", "Admin"

    role = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, choices=Role.choices, default=Role.CUSTOMER
    )

    @property
    def is_customer(self):
        return self.role == self.Role.CUSTOMER

    @property
    def is_support_staff(self):
        return self.role == self.Role.SUPPORT

    @property
    def is_helpdesk_admin(self):
        return self.role == self.Role.ADMIN or self.is_superuser

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.username} ({self.get_role_display()})"


========== accounts/permissions.py ==========

from rest_framework.permissions import SAFE_METHODS, BasePermission


def _logged_in(request):
    return bool(request.user and request.user.is_authenticated)


class IsAdmin(BasePermission):
    """Only Admin (or superuser)."""
    message = "Only admins can do this."

    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        return _logged_in(request) and request.user.is_helpdesk_admin


class IsSupportStaff(BasePermission):
    """Only Support Staff."""
    message = "Only support staff can do this."

    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        return _logged_in(request) and request.user.is_support_staff


class IsAdminOrSupport(BasePermission):
    """Admin or Support Staff (the 'staff' side of the helpdesk)."""
    message = "Only admin or support staff can do this."

    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        return _logged_in(request) and (
            request.user.is_helpdesk_admin or request.user.is_support_staff
        )


class IsCustomer(BasePermission):
    """Only End User / Customer."""
    message = "Only customers can do this."

    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        return _logged_in(request) and request.user.is_customer


class IsTicketParticipant(BasePermission):
    """
    Object-level rule for a Ticket:
      - Admin: full access
      - Support Staff: only tickets assigned to them
      - Customer: only tickets they created
    """
    message = "You do not have access to this ticket."

    def has_object_permission(self, request, view, obj):
        user = request.user
        if user.is_helpdesk_admin:
            return True
        if user.is_support_staff:
            return obj.assigned_to_id == user.id
        return obj.created_by_id == user.id


========== accounts/report_builders.py ==========

"""CSV / PDF file banane ke helpers (Django se independent)."""
import csv
import io
from xml.sax.saxutils import escape

# CSV me jo cell in characters se shuru ho, Excel use formula samajh leta hai.
_FORMULA_STARTS = ('=', '+', '-', '@', '\t', '\r')


def safe_cell(value):
    """CSV injection se bachav: formula jaise text ke aage ' laga do."""
    if value is None:
        return ''
    if isinstance(value, str) and value.startswith(_FORMULA_STARTS):
        return "'" + value
    return value


def csv_bytes(headers, rows):
    """UTF-8 (BOM ke saath, taaki Excel me sahi khule) CSV bytes."""
    buffer = io.StringIO()
    buffer.write('\ufeff')
    writer = csv.writer(buffer)
    writer.writerow(headers)
    for row in rows:
        writer.writerow([safe_cell(c) for c in row])
    return buffer.getvalue().encode('utf-8')


def pdf_bytes(title, subtitle, headers, rows, col_widths_mm):
    """
    Landscape A4 table PDF. `reportlab` chahiye (pip install reportlab).
    Note: default font sirf Latin (English) text dikhata hai.
    """
    from reportlab.lib import colors
    from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4, landscape
    from reportlab.lib.styles import ParagraphStyle, getSampleStyleSheet
    from reportlab.lib.units import mm
    from reportlab.platypus import (
        Paragraph, SimpleDocTemplate, Spacer, Table, TableStyle,
    )

    buffer = io.BytesIO()
    doc = SimpleDocTemplate(
        buffer, pagesize=landscape(A4),
        leftMargin=12 * mm, rightMargin=12 * mm,
        topMargin=12 * mm, bottomMargin=12 * mm,
        title=title,
    )
    styles = getSampleStyleSheet()
    cell_style = ParagraphStyle(
        'cell', parent=styles['BodyText'], fontSize=8, leading=10
    )
    head_style = ParagraphStyle(
        'head', parent=cell_style, textColor=colors.white,
        fontName='Helvetica-Bold',
    )

    data = [[Paragraph(escape(h), head_style) for h in headers]]
    for row in rows:
        data.append([
            Paragraph(escape('' if c is None else str(c)), cell_style)
            for c in row
        ])

    table = Table(
        data, colWidths=[w * mm for w in col_widths_mm], repeatRows=1
    )
    table.setStyle(TableStyle([
        ('BACKGROUND', (0, 0), (-1, 0), colors.HexColor('#1f4e79')),
        ('ROWBACKGROUNDS', (0, 1), (-1, -1),
         [colors.white, colors.HexColor('#f2f6fa')]),
        ('GRID', (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0.3, colors.HexColor('#b8c4d0')),
        ('VALIGN', (0, 0), (-1, -1), 'TOP'),
    ]))

    elements = [
        Paragraph(escape(title), styles['Title']),
        Paragraph(escape(subtitle), styles['Normal']),
        Spacer(1, 6 * mm),
        table,
    ]
    doc.build(elements)
    return buffer.getvalue()

    <div class="backdrop" id="backdrop"></div>

                    ticket</a>

                        id="theme-icon"></i></button>

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
    if (!Desk.getToken()) { window.location = '/login/'; }

    // Theme toggle
    const themeIcon = document.getElementById('theme-icon');
    function applyThemeIcon() {
        const t = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme');
        themeIcon.className = t === 'dark' ? 'bi bi-sun' : 'bi bi-moon-stars';
    }
    applyThemeIcon();
    document.getElementById('theme-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
        const next = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
        localStorage.setItem('deskly_theme', next);
        document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', next);
        document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-bs-theme', next);
        applyThemeIcon();
        window.dispatchEvent(new Event('themechange'));
    });

    // Mobile sidebar
    const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
    const backdrop = document.getElementById('backdrop');
    document.getElementById('menu-btn').addEventListener('click', () => { sidebar.classList.add('open'); backdrop.classList.add('show'); });
    backdrop.addEventListener('click', () => { sidebar.classList.remove('open'); backdrop.classList.remove('show'); });

    // Search -> tickets page
    document.getElementById('search-form').addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const q = document.getElementById('search-input').value.trim();
        window.location = '/tickets/' + (q ? '?search=' + encodeURIComponent(q) : '');
    });

    document.getElementById('logout-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
        try { await Desk.api('/logout/', { method: 'POST' }); } catch (e) { }
        Desk.logout();
    });

    // Logged-in user info (jo fields mile unhi se kaam chalega)
    const Me = { name: '', role: '' };
    (async () => {
        const res = await Desk.api('/user-profile/');
        if (res.ok && res.data) {
            const d = res.data;
            const full = [d.first_name, d.last_name].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
            Me.name = full || d.username || 'User';
            Me.role = (d.role || '').toString();
            document.getElementById('u-name').textContent = Me.name;
            document.getElementById('u-role').textContent = Me.role.replace('_', ' ');
            document.getElementById('u-avatar').textContent = Me.name.slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();
            if (Me.role === 'admin' || Me.role === 'support') {
                document.getElementById('staff-nav').style.display = 'block';
            }
            window.dispatchEvent(new Event('medata'));
        }
    })();
</script>

{% endblock %}

    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.3/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
    <script>
        // Shared helper: token + API calls (poori UI isi ko use karegi)
        const AUTH_PREFIX = 'Token ';   // agar API ka scheme alag hai to sirf yahan badlega
        const Desk = {
            getToken: () => localStorage.getItem('deskly_token'),
            setToken: (t) => localStorage.setItem('deskly_token', t),
            logout: () => { localStorage.removeItem('deskly_token'); window.location = '/login/'; },
            async api(path, options = {}) {
                const headers = Object.assign({ 'Accept': 'application/json' }, options.headers || {});
                const token = Desk.getToken();
                if (token) headers['Authorization'] = AUTH_PREFIX + token;
        const csrf = (document.cookie.match(/csrftoken=([^;]+)/) || [])[1];
        if (csrf) headers['X-CSRFToken'] = csrf;
                if (options.body && !(options.body instanceof FormData)) {
                    headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
                    options.body = JSON.stringify(options.body);
                }
                let res;
        try {
          res = await fetch('/api' + path, Object.assign({}, options, { headers }));
        } catch (e) {
          return { ok: false, status: 0, data: { detail: 'Cannot reach the server. Check that runserver is running.' } };
        }
                let data = null;
                try { data = await res.json(); } catch (e) { }
                if (res.status === 401) { Desk.logout(); }
                return { ok: res.ok, status: res.status, data };
            }
        };
    </script>
    {% block extra_js %}{% endblock %}
</body>

{% block page_js %}
<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js@4.4.3/dist/chart.umd.min.js"></script>
<script>
    const STATUS = { new: 'New', in_progress: 'In Progress', resolved: 'Resolved', closed: 'Closed' };
    const COLORS = { new: '#8b5cf6', in_progress: '#f59e0b', resolved: '#10b981', closed: '#64748b' };
    const css = (n) => getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(n).trim();
    const esc = (s) => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
    const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
    const key = (d) => d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.getDate());

    let tickets = [], counts = { total: 0, new: 0, in_progress: 0, resolved: 0, closed: 0 };
    let charts = {}, mainKind = 'opened';

    function lastDays(n) {
        const out = [];
        for (let i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--) { const d = new Date(); d.setDate(d.getDate() - i); out.push(d); }
        return out;
    }
    function series(list, field, n) {
        const days = lastDays(n), map = {};
        days.forEach(d => map[key(d)] = 0);
        list.forEach(t => { if (t[field]) { const k = key(new Date(t[field])); if (k in map) map[k]++; } });
        return { labels: days.map(d => d.toLocaleDateString('en', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })), data: days.map(d => map[key(d)]) };
    }
    function lastWeek(list, field) {
        const since = Date.now() - 7 * 864e5;
        return list.filter(t => t[field] && new Date(t[field]).getTime() >= since).length;
    }
    function killChart(id) { if (charts[id]) { charts[id].destroy(); delete charts[id]; } }

    function renderStats() {
        const defs = [
            { k: 'total', label: 'Total tickets', icon: 'bi-collection', color: '#10b981', list: tickets, field: 'created_at' },
            { k: 'new', label: 'New', icon: 'bi-stars', color: '#8b5cf6', list: tickets.filter(t => t.status === 'new'), field: 'created_at' },
            { k: 'in_progress', label: 'In progress', icon: 'bi-hourglass-split', color: '#f59e0b', list: tickets.filter(t => t.status === 'in_progress'), field: 'created_at' },
            { k: 'resolved', label: 'Resolved', icon: 'bi-check2-circle', color: '#10b981', list: tickets.filter(t => t.status === 'resolved' || t.status === 'closed'), field: 'resolved_at' },
        ];
        const grid = document.getElementById('stat-grid');
        grid.innerHTML = defs.map(d => `
      <div class="cardx stat">
        <div class="label">${d.label}</div>
        <div class="value">${counts[d.k]}</div>
        <div class="delta" style="color:${d.color}"><i class="bi bi-graph-up-arrow"></i> ${lastWeek(d.list, d.field)} in last 7 days</div>
        <div class="ico" style="background:${d.color}22;color:${d.color}"><i class="bi ${d.icon}"></i></div>
        <canvas id="spark-${d.k}"></canvas>
      </div>`).join('');
        defs.forEach(d => {
            killChart('spark-' + d.k);
            const s = series(d.list, d.field, 14);
            charts['spark-' + d.k] = new Chart(document.getElementById('spark-' + d.k), {
                type: 'line',
                data: {
                    labels: s.labels, datasets: [{
                        data: s.data, borderColor: d.color, borderWidth: 2, tension: .4, pointRadius: 0, fill: true,
                        backgroundColor: (c) => { const g = c.chart.ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 70); g.addColorStop(0, d.color + '44'); g.addColorStop(1, d.color + '00'); return g; }
                    }]
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, plugins: { legend: { display: false }, tooltip: { enabled: false } },
                    scales: { x: { display: false }, y: { display: false, beginAtZero: true } }, layout: { padding: 0 }
                }
            });
        });
    }

    function renderMain() {
        killChart('main');
        const field = mainKind === 'opened' ? 'created_at' : 'resolved_at';
        const s = series(tickets, field, 30);
        const accent = css('--accent');
        charts.main = new Chart(document.getElementById('mainChart'), {
            type: 'line',
            data: {
                labels: s.labels, datasets: [{
                    label: mainKind === 'opened' ? 'Opened' : 'Resolved', data: s.data, borderColor: accent, borderWidth: 2.5, tension: .4, pointRadius: 0, pointHoverRadius: 5, fill: true,
                    backgroundColor: (c) => { const g = c.chart.ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 290); g.addColorStop(0, accent + '55'); g.addColorStop(1, accent + '00'); return g; }
                }]
            },
            options: {
                responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false, interaction: { intersect: false, mode: 'index' },
                plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
                scales: {
                    x: { grid: { display: false }, ticks: { color: css('--muted'), maxTicksLimit: 8, font: { size: 11 } }, border: { display: false } },
                    y: { beginAtZero: true, grid: { color: css('--grid') }, ticks: { color: css('--muted'), precision: 0, font: { size: 11 } }, border: { display: false } }
                }
            }
        });
    }

    function renderDonut() {
        killChart('donut');
        const keys = ['new', 'in_progress', 'resolved', 'closed'];
        document.getElementById('donut-total').textContent = counts.total;
        charts.donut = new Chart(document.getElementById('donut'), {
            type: 'doughnut',
            data: { labels: keys.map(k => STATUS[k]), datasets: [{ data: keys.map(k => counts[k]), backgroundColor: keys.map(k => COLORS[k]), borderColor: css('--card'), borderWidth: 3 }] },
            options: { cutout: '72%', plugins: { legend: { display: false } }, maintainAspectRatio: false }
        });
        document.getElementById('legend').innerHTML = keys.map(k => `
      <div class="legend-row"><span class="dot" style="background:${COLORS[k]}"></span>${STATUS[k]}<span class="n">${counts[k]}</span></div>`).join('');
    }

    function renderPriority() {
        const m = {};
        tickets.forEach(t => m[t.priority] = (m[t.priority] || 0) + 1);
        const rows = Object.entries(m).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
        const max = rows.length ? rows[0][1] : 1;
        document.getElementById('prio-bars').innerHTML = rows.length ? rows.map(([p, n]) => `
      <div class="bar-row"><div class="top"><span>${esc(p.replace('_', ' '))}</span><span>${n}</span></div>
      <div class="bar-track"><div class="bar-fill" style="width:${(n / max) * 100}%"></div></div></div>`).join('')
            : '<div class="muted">No tickets yet.</div>';
    }

    function renderRecent() {
        const body = document.getElementById('recent-body');
        if (!tickets.length) { body.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6" class="muted">No tickets yet.</td></tr>'; return; }
        body.innerHTML = tickets.slice(0, 8).map(t => `
      <tr>
        <td class="muted">#${t.id}</td>
        <td><a href="/tickets/${t.id}/">${esc(t.title)}</a></td>
        <td><span class="pill p-${t.status}">${STATUS[t.status] || esc(t.status)}</span></td>
        <td style="text-transform:capitalize">${esc((t.priority || '').replace('_', ' '))}</td>
        <td>${esc(t.assigned_to || 'â€”')}</td>
        <td class="muted">${new Date(t.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })}</td>
      </tr>`).join('');
    }

    function renderAll() { renderStats(); renderMain(); renderDonut(); renderPriority(); renderRecent(); }

    async function load() {
        const [list, ...cs] = await Promise.all([
            Desk.api('/tickets/?page_size=100&ordering=-created_at'),
            Desk.api('/tickets/?page_size=1'),
            Desk.api('/tickets/?status=new&page_size=1'),
            Desk.api('/tickets/?status=in_progress&page_size=1'),
            Desk.api('/tickets/?status=resolved&page_size=1'),
            Desk.api('/tickets/?status=closed&page_size=1'),
        ]);
        tickets = (list.data && list.data.results) || [];
        const n = (r) => (r.data && r.data.count) || 0;
        counts = { total: n(cs[0]), new: n(cs[1]), in_progress: n(cs[2]), resolved: n(cs[3]), closed: n(cs[4]) };
        renderAll();
    }

    document.getElementById('seg').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        const b = e.target.closest('button'); if (!b) return;
        document.querySelectorAll('#seg button').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('on'));
        b.classList.add('on'); mainKind = b.dataset.k; renderMain();
    });
    window.addEventListener('themechange', renderAll);
    window.addEventListener('medata', () => {
        document.getElementById('welcome').textContent = 'Welcome back, ' + Me.name + '. Here is what is happening with your tickets today.';
    });
    load();
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
    if (Desk.getToken()) { window.location = '/dashboard/'; }

    const form = document.getElementById('login-form');
    const errorBox = document.getElementById('error-box');
    const btn = document.getElementById('submit-btn');

    document.getElementById('pw-toggle').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        const pw = document.getElementById('password');
        const show = pw.type === 'password';
        pw.type = show ? 'text' : 'password';
        e.target.textContent = show ? 'Hide' : 'Show';
    });

    function showError(msg) { errorBox.textContent = msg; errorBox.style.display = 'block'; }

    form.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        errorBox.style.display = 'none';
        const username = document.getElementById('username').value.trim();
        const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
        if (!username || !password) { showError('Please enter your username and password.'); return; }

        btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Signing in...';
        const res = await Desk.api('/login/', { method: 'POST', body: { username, password } });
        btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Sign in';

        if (res.ok && res.data) {
            const token = res.data.token || res.data.key || res.data.access;
            if (token) { Desk.setToken(token); window.location = '/dashboard/'; return; }
        }
        const d = res.data || {};
        showError(d.detail || d.error || d.non_field_errors?.[0] || 'Login failed. Check your details and try again.');
    });
</script>
{% endblock %}

        </div>
    </form>

{% block page_js %}
<script>
    const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
    const esc = (s) => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
    const listOf = (d) => Array.isArray(d) ? d : ((d && d.results) || []);
    const selectedTags = new Set();
    let chosenFiles = [];

    function showMsg(text) { $('msg').textContent = text; $('msg').style.display = 'block'; window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' }); }
    function flatten(d) {
        if (!d) return 'Something went wrong.';
        if (typeof d === 'string') return d;
        return Object.entries(d).map(([k, v]) => (k === 'detail' || k === 'non_field_errors' ? '' : k + ': ') + [].concat(v).join(' ')).join(' | ');
    }

    async function init() {
        const [pr, ca, tg] = await Promise.all([Desk.api('/priorities/'), Desk.api('/admin/categories/'), Desk.api('/admin/tags/')]);
        const prios = listOf(pr.data).filter(p => p.slug && p.is_active !== false);
        $('priority').innerHTML = prios.map(p => `<option value="${esc(p.slug)}">${esc(p.name)}</option>`).join('') || '<option value="medium">Medium</option>';
        if (prios.some(p => p.slug === 'medium')) $('priority').value = 'medium';
        listOf(ca.data).forEach(c => $('category').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', `<option value="${c.id}">${esc(c.name)}</option>`));
        const tags = listOf(tg.data);
        if (tags.length) {
            $('tags').innerHTML = tags.map(t => `<button type="button" class="chipx" data-id="${t.id}">${esc(t.name)}</button>`).join('');
        }
    }

    $('tags').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        const b = e.target.closest('.chipx'); if (!b) return;
        const id = Number(b.dataset.id);
        if (selectedTags.has(id)) { selectedTags.delete(id); b.classList.remove('on'); } else { selectedTags.add(id); b.classList.add('on'); }
    });

    $('drop').addEventListener('click', () => $('files').click());
    $('files').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        chosenFiles = Array.from(e.target.files);
        $('file-list').innerHTML = chosenFiles.map(f => `<div><i class="bi bi-paperclip"></i> ${esc(f.name)} (${(f.size / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB)</div>`).join('');
    });

    $('form').addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        $('msg').style.display = 'none';
        const title = $('title').value.trim(), description = $('description').value.trim();
        if (!title || !description) { showMsg('Title and description are required.'); return; }

        const body = { title, description, priority: $('priority').value, tags: Array.from(selectedTags) };
        if ($('category').value) body.category = Number($('category').value);

        $('submit').disabled = true;
        const res = await Desk.api('/my-tickets/', { method: 'POST', body });
        if (!res.ok) { $('submit').disabled = false; showMsg(flatten(res.data)); return; }

        const id = res.data.id;
        if (chosenFiles.length) {
            const fd = new FormData();
            chosenFiles.forEach(f => fd.append('files', f));
            const up = await Desk.api('/tickets/' + id + '/attachments/', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
            if (!up.ok) { alert('Ticket created, but files were not uploaded: ' + flatten(up.data)); }
        }
        window.location = '/tickets/' + id + '/';
    });

    init();
</script>
{% endblock %}

                <div id="staff-box" style="display:none" class="mt-3">
                    <label class="muted"
                        style="font-size:.72rem;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.1em;font-weight:600">Update
                        status</label>
                    <div class="d-flex gap-2 mt-1">
                        <select class="fx" id="s-select">
                            <option value="new">New</option>
                            <option value="in_progress">In progress</option>
                            <option value="resolved">Resolved</option>
                            <option value="closed">Closed</option>
                        </select>
                        <button class="btn-accent" id="s-save" type="button">Save</button>
                    </div>
                    <div class="notice" id="s-msg"></div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}

{% block page_js %}
<script>
    const TICKET_ID = parseInt("{{ ticket_id }}", 10);
    const STATUS = { new: 'New', in_progress: 'In Progress', resolved: 'Resolved', closed: 'Closed' };
    const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
    const esc = (s) => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
    const listOf = (d) => Array.isArray(d) ? d : ((d && d.results) || []);
    const fmt = (v) => v ? new Date(v).toLocaleString('en', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }) : 'â€”';
    const flatten = (d) => !d ? 'Something went wrong.' : (typeof d === 'string' ? d : Object.values(d).flat().join(' '));
    function note(el, text, kind) { el.textContent = text; el.className = 'notice ' + kind; el.style.display = 'block'; }

    let ticketUrl = '/my-tickets/' + TICKET_ID + '/';
    let isStaff = false;

    function whenMe(cb) { if (Me.role) cb(); else window.addEventListener('medata', cb, { once: true }); }

    function render(t) {
        $('t-title').textContent = t.title;
        $('t-status').textContent = STATUS[t.status] || t.status;
        $('t-status').className = 'pill p-' + t.status;
        $('t-desc').textContent = t.description || '';
        const tags = t.tag_names || [];
        $('t-tags').innerHTML = tags.map(n => '<span class="tag">' + esc(n) + '</span>').join('');
        $('d-id').textContent = t.id;
        $('d-priority').textContent = (t.priority || 'â€”').replace('_', ' ');
        $('d-category').textContent = t.category_name || 'â€”';
        $('d-by').textContent = t.created_by || 'â€”';
        $('d-assigned').textContent = t.assigned_to || 'Unassigned';
        $('d-created').textContent = fmt(t.created_at);
        $('d-updated').textContent = fmt(t.updated_at);
        $('s-select').value = t.status;
        $('loading').style.display = 'none';
        $('view').style.display = 'block';
    }

    async function loadTicket() {
        const role = Me.role;
        isStaff = role === 'admin' || role === 'support';
        if (role === 'admin') ticketUrl = '/admin/tickets/' + TICKET_ID + '/';
        else if (role === 'support') ticketUrl = '/support/tickets/' + TICKET_ID + '/';
        let res = await Desk.api(ticketUrl);
        if (!res.ok && ticketUrl !== '/my-tickets/' + TICKET_ID + '/') {
            const alt = await Desk.api('/my-tickets/' + TICKET_ID + '/');
            if (alt.ok) { res = alt; ticketUrl = '/my-tickets/' + TICKET_ID + '/'; }
        }
        if (!res.ok) {
            $('loading').style.display = 'none';
            $('error').style.display = 'block';
            $('error').innerHTML = '<h3>Ticket not found</h3><div class="muted mt-1">' + esc(res.status === 404 ? 'It does not exist, or you do not have access to it.' : flatten(res.data)) + '</div>';
            return;
        }
        render(res.data);
        $('staff-box').style.display = isStaff ? 'block' : 'none';
        $('int-wrap').style.display = isStaff ? 'block' : 'none';
        loadComments(); loadFiles();
    }

    async function loadComments() {
        const res = await Desk.api('/tickets/' + TICKET_ID + '/comments/');
        const items = listOf(res.data);
        if (!res.ok) { $('comments').innerHTML = '<div class="muted mt-2">Could not load comments.</div>'; return; }
        $('comments').innerHTML = items.length ? items.map(c => {
            const who = c.author_name || c.author || 'User';
            return `<div class="cmt"><div class="avatar">${esc(String(who).slice(0, 2).toUpperCase())}</div>
        <div class="body"><div class="who">${esc(who)}${c.is_internal ? '<span class="badge-int">Internal</span>' : ''}<span class="when">${fmt(c.created_at)}</span></div>
        <div class="txt ${c.is_internal ? 'internal' : ''}">${esc(c.body)}</div></div></div>`;
        }).join('') : '<div class="muted mt-2">No messages yet. Start the conversation below.</div>';
    }

    async function loadFiles() {
        const res = await Desk.api('/tickets/' + TICKET_ID + '/attachments/');
        const items = listOf(res.data);
        if (!res.ok) { $('files').innerHTML = '<div class="muted mt-2">Could not load attachments.</div>'; return; }
        $('files').innerHTML = items.length ? items.map(f => `
      <div class="file-row"><i class="bi bi-paperclip"></i>
        <a href="${esc(f.url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${esc(f.filename)}</a>
        <span class="muted ms-auto" style="font-size:.78rem">${esc(f.uploaded_by || '')}</span></div>`).join('')
            : '<div class="muted mt-2">No files attached.</div>';
    }

    $('c-form').addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const body = $('c-body').value.trim();
        if (!body) { note($('c-err'), 'Please write a message first.', 'err'); return; }
        const res = await Desk.api('/tickets/' + TICKET_ID + '/comments/', { method: 'POST', body: { body, is_internal: isStaff && $('c-internal').checked } });
        if (!res.ok) { note($('c-err'), flatten(res.data), 'err'); return; }
        $('c-err').style.display = 'none'; $('c-body').value = ''; $('c-internal').checked = false;
        loadComments();
    });

    $('f-upload').addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const files = Array.from($('f-input').files);
        if (!files.length) { note($('f-err'), 'Choose at least one file.', 'err'); return; }
        const fd = new FormData(); files.forEach(f => fd.append('files', f));
        const res = await Desk.api('/tickets/' + TICKET_ID + '/attachments/', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
        if (!res.ok) { note($('f-err'), flatten(res.data), 'err'); return; }
        $('f-err').style.display = 'none'; $('f-input').value = '';
        loadFiles();
    });

    $('s-save').addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const status = $('s-select').value;
        const res = await Desk.api(ticketUrl, { method: 'PATCH', body: { status } });
        if (!res.ok) { note($('s-msg'), flatten(res.data), 'err'); return; }
        note($('s-msg'), 'Status updated.', 'ok');
        $('t-status').textContent = STATUS[status]; $('t-status').className = 'pill p-' + status;
    });

    whenMe(loadTicket);
</script>
{% endblock %}


========== accounts/templates/ui/tickets.html ==========

{% block page_js %}
<script>
    const STATUS = { new: 'New', in_progress: 'In Progress', resolved: 'Resolved', closed: 'Closed' };
    const PAGE_SIZE = 10;
    const qs = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    const state = {
        page: parseInt(qs.get('page') || '1', 10), search: qs.get('search') || '', status: qs.get('status') || '',
        priority: qs.get('priority') || '', category: qs.get('category') || '', tag: qs.get('tag') || '',
        created_from: qs.get('created_from') || '', created_to: qs.get('created_to') || '',
        ordering: qs.get('ordering') || '-created_at',
    };
    const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
    const esc = (s) => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
    const listOf = (d) => Array.isArray(d) ? d : ((d && d.results) || []);

    function query() {
        const p = new URLSearchParams();
        Object.entries(state).forEach(([k, v]) => { if (v && !(k === 'page' && v === 1)) p.set(k, v); });
        return p;
    }

    function fillSelect(id, items, valueKey, labelKey, current) {
        const sel = $(id);
        items.forEach(it => {
            const o = document.createElement('option');
            o.value = it[valueKey]; o.textContent = it[labelKey];
            sel.appendChild(o);
        });
        sel.value = current || '';
    }

    async function loadFilters() {
        const [pr, ca, tg] = await Promise.all([Desk.api('/priorities/'), Desk.api('/admin/categories/'), Desk.api('/admin/tags/')]);
        fillSelect('f-priority', listOf(pr.data).filter(x => x.slug), 'slug', 'name', state.priority);
        fillSelect('f-category', listOf(ca.data), 'id', 'name', state.category);
        fillSelect('f-tag', listOf(tg.data), 'name', 'name', state.tag);
    }

    function syncInputs() {
        $('f-search').value = state.search; $('f-status').value = state.status; $('f-ordering').value = state.ordering;
        $('f-from').value = state.created_from; $('f-to').value = state.created_to;
        const top = $('search-input'); if (top) top.value = state.search;
    }

    function render(data) {
        const rows = data.results || [];
        $('count-label').textContent = data.count + (data.count === 1 ? ' ticket found' : ' tickets found');
        if (!rows.length) {
            $('rows').innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7"><div class="empty"><i class="bi bi-inbox"></i>No tickets match these filters.</div></td></tr>';
        } else {
            $('rows').innerHTML = rows.map(t => `
        <tr onclick="window.location='/tickets/${t.id}/'">
          <td class="muted">#${t.id}</td>
          <td><div class="ttl">${esc(t.title)}</div>
              <div class="sub">${esc(t.description_preview)}</div>
              <div class="mt-1">${(t.tag_names || []).map(n => '<span class="tag">' + esc(n) + '</span>').join('')}</div></td>
          <td><span class="pill p-${t.status}">${STATUS[t.status] || esc(t.status)}</span></td>
          <td><span class="prio ${esc(t.priority)}"><i></i>${esc((t.priority || '').replace('_', ' '))}</span></td>
          <td>${esc(t.category_name || 'â€”')}</td>
          <td>${esc(t.assigned_to || 'â€”')}</td>
          <td class="muted">${new Date(t.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' })}</td>
        </tr>`).join('');
        }
        const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(data.count / PAGE_SIZE));
        const from = data.count ? (state.page - 1) * PAGE_SIZE + 1 : 0;
        $('page-info').textContent = data.count ? `Showing ${from}-${Math.min(state.page * PAGE_SIZE, data.count)} of ${data.count}` : '';
        let html = `<button class="pg" ${state.page <= 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} data-p="${state.page - 1}">Prev</button>`;
        const start = Math.max(1, state.page - 2), end = Math.min(pages, start + 4);
        for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) html += `<button class="pg ${i === state.page ? 'on' : ''}" data-p="${i}">${i}</button>`;
        html += `<button class="pg" ${state.page >= pages ? 'disabled' : ''} data-p="${state.page + 1}">Next</button>`;
        $('pager').innerHTML = pages > 1 ? html : '';
    }

    async function load() {
        const q = query(); q.set('page_size', PAGE_SIZE);
        history.replaceState(null, '', '/tickets/' + (query().toString() ? '?' + query().toString() : ''));
        const res = await Desk.api('/tickets/?' + q.toString());
        if (res.ok) { render(res.data); }
        else {
            const msg = res.data ? (Object.values(res.data).flat().join(' ') || 'Something went wrong.') : 'Something went wrong.';
            $('rows').innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7"><div class="empty"><i class="bi bi-exclamation-circle"></i>' + esc(msg) + '</div></td></tr>';
            $('count-label').textContent = 'Could not load tickets';
        }
    }

    const bind = {
        'f-status': 'status', 'f-priority': 'priority', 'f-category': 'category', 'f-tag': 'tag',
        'f-ordering': 'ordering', 'f-from': 'created_from', 'f-to': 'created_to'
    };
    Object.entries(bind).forEach(([id, key]) => $(id).addEventListener('change', (e) => { state[key] = e.target.value; state.page = 1; load(); }));

    let timer;
    $('f-search').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
        clearTimeout(timer);
        timer = setTimeout(() => { state.search = e.target.value.trim(); state.page = 1; load(); }, 350);
    });
    $('f-clear').addEventListener('click', () => {
        Object.assign(state, { page: 1, search: '', status: '', priority: '', category: '', tag: '', created_from: '', created_to: '', ordering: '-created_at' });
        ['f-priority', 'f-category', 'f-tag'].forEach(id => $(id).value = '');
        syncInputs(); load();
    });
    $('pager').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        const b = e.target.closest('button'); if (!b || b.disabled) return;
        state.page = parseInt(b.dataset.p, 10); load(); window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    });

    syncInputs();
    loadFilters().then(load);
</script>
{% endblock %}


========== accounts/tests.py ==========

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.urls import reverse
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.test import APITestCase
from rest_framework.authtoken.models import Token

User = get_user_model()


class AuthTests(APITestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.username = 'testuser'
        self.password = 'password123'
        self.user = User.objects.create_user(
            username=self.username,
            password=self.password,
            email='test@example.com'
        )
        self.login_url = reverse('login')
        self.logout_url = reverse('logout')

    def test_login_success(self):
        data = {'username': self.username, 'password': self.password}
        response = self.client.post(self.login_url, data, format='json')
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertIn('token', response.data)
        self.assertEqual(response.data['user']['username'], self.username)

    def test_login_invalid_credentials(self):
        data = {'username': self.username, 'password': 'wrongpassword'}
        response = self.client.post(self.login_url, data, format='json')
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED)
        self.assertIn('error', response.data)

    def test_logout_authenticated(self):
        # Create token and authenticate client
        token = Token.objects.create(user=self.user)
        self.client.credentials(HTTP_AUTHORIZATION='Token ' + token.key)

        response = self.client.post(self.logout_url, format='json')
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        # Verify token was deleted
        self.assertFalse(Token.objects.filter(user=self.user).exists())

    def test_logout_unauthenticated(self):
        response = self.client.post(self.logout_url, format='json')
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED)



========== accounts/ui_urls.py ==========

from django.urls import path

from accounts.ui_views import (
    DashboardPageView,
    LoginPageView,
    NewTicketPageView,
    TicketDetailPageView,
    TicketsPageView,
)

urlpatterns = [
    path('', LoginPageView.as_view(), name='ui-home'),
    path('login/', LoginPageView.as_view(), name='ui-login'),
    path('dashboard/', DashboardPageView.as_view(), name='ui-dashboard'),
    path('tickets/', TicketsPageView.as_view(), name='ui-tickets'),
    path('tickets/new/', NewTicketPageView.as_view(), name='ui-ticket-new'),
    path('tickets/<int:pk>/', TicketDetailPageView.as_view(), name='ui-ticket-detail'),

========== accounts/ui_views.py ==========

from django.utils.decorators import method_decorator
from django.views.decorators.csrf import ensure_csrf_cookie
from django.views.generic import TemplateView


@method_decorator(ensure_csrf_cookie, name='dispatch')
class LoginPageView(TemplateView):
    template_name = 'ui/login.html'


@method_decorator(ensure_csrf_cookie, name='dispatch')
class DashboardPageView(TemplateView):
    template_name = 'ui/dashboard.html'


@method_decorator(ensure_csrf_cookie, name='dispatch')
class TicketsPageView(TemplateView):
    template_name = 'ui/tickets.html'


@method_decorator(ensure_csrf_cookie, name='dispatch')
class NewTicketPageView(TemplateView):
    template_name = 'ui/new_ticket.html'


@method_decorator(ensure_csrf_cookie, name='dispatch')
class TicketDetailPageView(TemplateView):
    template_name = 'ui/ticket_detail.html'

    def get_context_data(self, **kwargs):
        context = super().get_context_data(**kwargs)
        context['ticket_id'] = int(kwargs['pk'])
        return context
