========== accounts/views/admin_ticket_detail_view.py ==========

from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import generics, serializers

from accounts.permissions import IsAdmin
from tickets.models import Ticket, TicketHistory


class AdminTicketDetailSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    created_by = serializers.CharField(source='created_by.username', read_only=True)
    assigned_to = serializers.CharField(
        source='assigned_to.username', read_only=True, default=None
    )

    class Meta:
        model = Ticket
        fields = [
            'id', 'title', 'description', 'category', 'priority', 'status',
            'created_by', 'assigned_to', 'created_at', 'updated_at', 'resolved_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = [
            'id', 'created_by', 'assigned_to',
            'created_at', 'updated_at', 'resolved_at',
        ]


class AdminTicketDetailView(generics.RetrieveUpdateDestroyAPIView):
    """
    GET    /api/admin/tickets/<id>/   -> full ticket details
    PATCH  /api/admin/tickets/<id>/   -> title, description, category, priority, status
    DELETE /api/admin/tickets/<id>/   -> ticket (comments/history ke saath) delete

    Assign karne ke liye /api/admin/tickets/<id>/assign/ use karo.
    Admin status ko kisi bhi value par set kar sakta hai (flow rules support staff
    ke liye hain). Status/priority badalne par history me entry banti hai.
    """
    queryset = Ticket.objects.select_related('created_by', 'assigned_to')
    serializer_class = AdminTicketDetailSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAdmin]
    http_method_names = ['get', 'patch', 'delete', 'head', 'options']

    def perform_update(self, serializer):
        ticket = serializer.instance
        old_status, old_priority = ticket.status, ticket.priority
        new_status = serializer.validated_data.get('status', old_status)

        extra = {}
        if new_status == Ticket.Status.RESOLVED and old_status != Ticket.Status.RESOLVED:
            extra['resolved_at'] = timezone.now()
        elif (new_status in (Ticket.Status.NEW, Ticket.Status.IN_PROGRESS)
              and old_status in (Ticket.Status.RESOLVED, Ticket.Status.CLOSED)):
            extra['resolved_at'] = None  # ticket dobara khul gaya

        ticket = serializer.save(**extra)

        user = self.request.user
        if ticket.status != old_status:
            TicketHistory.objects.create(
                ticket=ticket, changed_by=user, field='status',
                old_value=old_status, new_value=ticket.status,
            )
        if ticket.priority != old_priority:
            TicketHistory.objects.create(
                ticket=ticket, changed_by=user, field='priority',
                old_value=old_priority, new_value=ticket.priority,
            )


========== accounts/views/admin_ticket_views.py ==========

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.db.models import Count, Q
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404
from rest_framework import generics, serializers, status
from rest_framework.exceptions import ValidationError
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from accounts.permissions import IsAdmin
from tickets.models import Ticket, TicketHistory

User = get_user_model()


class AdminTicketSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    created_by = serializers.CharField(source='created_by.username', read_only=True)
    assigned_to = serializers.CharField(
        source='assigned_to.username', read_only=True, default=None
    )
    assigned_to_id = serializers.IntegerField(read_only=True)
    category_name = serializers.CharField(
        source='category.name', read_only=True, default=None
    )

    class Meta:
        model = Ticket
        fields = [
            'id', 'title', 'priority', 'status', 'category', 'category_name',
            'created_by', 'assigned_to', 'assigned_to_id',
            'created_at', 'updated_at', 'resolved_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = fields


class AssignSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    # null bhejne par ticket unassign ho jata hai
    assigned_to = serializers.PrimaryKeyRelatedField(
        queryset=User.objects.filter(role='support', is_active=True),
        allow_null=True,
    )


def assign_ticket(ticket, staff, changed_by):
    """Ticket ko staff (ya None) ko assign karo aur history me likho."""
    if ticket.status == Ticket.Status.CLOSED:
        raise ValidationError('A closed ticket cannot be reassigned.')

    old = ticket.assigned_to
    if (old and staff and old.pk == staff.pk) or (old is None and staff is None):
        return ticket

    ticket.assigned_to = staff
    ticket.save(update_fields=['assigned_to', 'updated_at'])

    TicketHistory.objects.create(
        ticket=ticket,
        changed_by=changed_by,
        field='assigned_to',
        old_value=old.username if old else '-',
        new_value=staff.username if staff else '-',
    )
    return ticket


class AdminTicketListView(generics.ListAPIView):
    """
    GET /api/admin/tickets/   -> all tickets
    Filters: ?status=new  ?priority=high  ?category=1
             ?assigned_to=<user id> or ?assigned_to=unassigned
             ?search=<id or title>
    """
    serializer_class = AdminTicketSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAdmin]

    def get_queryset(self):
        qs = Ticket.objects.select_related('category', 'created_by', 'assigned_to')
        params = self.request.query_params

        if params.get('status'):
            qs = qs.filter(status=params['status'])
        if params.get('priority'):
            qs = qs.filter(priority=params['priority'])
        if params.get('category'):
            qs = qs.filter(category_id=params['category'])

        assigned = params.get('assigned_to')
        if assigned == 'unassigned':
            qs = qs.filter(assigned_to__isnull=True)
        elif assigned and assigned.isdigit():
            qs = qs.filter(assigned_to_id=int(assigned))

        search = params.get('search')
        if search:
            condition = Q(title__icontains=search)
            if search.isdigit():
                condition |= Q(pk=int(search))
            qs = qs.filter(condition)

        return qs


class AdminAssignTicketView(APIView):
    """
    PATCH /api/admin/tickets/<id>/assign/   (MANUAL assignment)
    Body: {"assigned_to": <support user id>}   or   {"assigned_to": null}
    """
    permission_classes = [IsAdmin]

    def patch(self, request, pk, *args, **kwargs):
        ticket = get_object_or_404(Ticket, pk=pk)
        serializer = AssignSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        assign_ticket(ticket, serializer.validated_data['assigned_to'], request.user)
        return Response(AdminTicketSerializer(ticket).data)


class AdminAutoAssignTicketView(APIView):
    """
    POST /api/admin/tickets/<id>/auto-assign/   (AUTO assignment)
    Ticket us active support staff ko milta hai jiske paas
    sabse kam open (New / In Progress) tickets hain.
    """
    permission_classes = [IsAdmin]

    def post(self, request, pk, *args, **kwargs):
        ticket = get_object_or_404(Ticket, pk=pk)

        staff = (
            User.objects.filter(role='support', is_active=True)
            .annotate(
                open_count=Count(
                    'assigned_tickets',
                    filter=Q(assigned_tickets__status__in=[
                        Ticket.Status.NEW, Ticket.Status.IN_PROGRESS,
                    ]),
                )
            )
            .order_by('open_count', 'id')
            .first()
        )
        if staff is None:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'No active support staff available.'},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        assign_ticket(ticket, staff, request.user)
        return Response(AdminTicketSerializer(ticket).data)


========== accounts/views/admin_views.py ==========

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model, password_validation
from rest_framework import generics, serializers
from rest_framework.exceptions import ValidationError
from rest_framework.permissions import SAFE_METHODS, BasePermission, IsAuthenticated

from accounts.permissions import IsAdmin
from tickets.models import Category

User = get_user_model()


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ users
class AdminUserSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    password = serializers.CharField(write_only=True, required=False)

    class Meta:
        model = User
        fields = [
            'id', 'username', 'email', 'first_name', 'last_name',
            'role', 'is_active', 'password', 'date_joined',
        ]
        read_only_fields = ['id', 'date_joined']

    def validate_password(self, value):
        password_validation.validate_password(value)
        return value

    def validate(self, attrs):
        if self.instance is None and not attrs.get('password'):
            raise serializers.ValidationError(
                {'password': 'Password is required when creating a user.'}
            )
        return attrs

    def create(self, validated_data):
        password = validated_data.pop('password')
        return User.objects.create_user(password=password, **validated_data)

    def update(self, instance, validated_data):
        password = validated_data.pop('password', None)
        for field, value in validated_data.items():
            setattr(instance, field, value)
        if password:
            instance.set_password(password)
        instance.save()
        return instance


class AdminUserListCreateView(generics.ListCreateAPIView):
    """
    GET  /api/admin/users/   -> list users (?role=support&is_active=true&search=ali)
    POST /api/admin/users/   -> create a user with a role (Admin only)
    """
    serializer_class = AdminUserSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAdmin]

    def get_queryset(self):
        qs = User.objects.all().order_by('id')
        params = self.request.query_params

        role = params.get('role')
        is_active = params.get('is_active')
        search = params.get('search')

        if role:
            qs = qs.filter(role=role)
        if is_active in ('true', 'false'):
            qs = qs.filter(is_active=(is_active == 'true'))
        if search:
            qs = qs.filter(username__icontains=search) | qs.filter(
                email__icontains=search
            )
        return qs


class AdminUserDetailView(generics.RetrieveUpdateAPIView):
    """
    GET   /api/admin/users/<id>/  -> user details
    PATCH /api/admin/users/<id>/  -> change role / deactivate / edit details
    Users are deactivated (is_active=false), never deleted.
    """
    serializer_class = AdminUserSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAdmin]
    queryset = User.objects.all()
    http_method_names = ['get', 'patch', 'head', 'options']

    def perform_update(self, serializer):
        instance = serializer.instance
        data = serializer.validated_data

        # Admin apne aap ko lock-out na kar le
        if instance.pk == self.request.user.pk:
            if data.get('is_active') is False:
                raise ValidationError('You cannot deactivate your own account.')
            new_role = data.get('role')
            if new_role and new_role != instance.role and instance.role == 'admin':
                raise ValidationError('You cannot remove your own admin role.')

        serializer.save()


# ------------------------------------------------------------- categories
class IsAdminOrReadOnly(BasePermission):
    """Everyone logged in can read categories; only Admin can change them."""

    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        user = request.user
        if not (user and user.is_authenticated):
            return False
        if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:
            return True
        return user.is_helpdesk_admin


class CategorySerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = Category
        fields = ['id', 'name']


class AdminCategoryListCreateView(generics.ListCreateAPIView):
    """
    GET  /api/admin/categories/  -> any logged-in user (needed to create tickets)
    POST /api/admin/categories/  -> Admin only
    """
    queryset = Category.objects.all()
    serializer_class = CategorySerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAdminOrReadOnly]


class AdminCategoryDetailView(generics.RetrieveUpdateDestroyAPIView):
    """GET for all logged-in users; PATCH/DELETE for Admin only."""
    queryset = Category.objects.all()
    serializer_class = CategorySerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAdminOrReadOnly]


========== accounts/views/attachment_views.py ==========

import os

from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404
from rest_framework import serializers, status
from rest_framework.exceptions import PermissionDenied, ValidationError
from rest_framework.parsers import FormParser, MultiPartParser
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from accounts.permissions import IsTicketParticipant
from tickets.models import Attachment, Ticket
from tickets.site_settings import SystemSettings


class AttachmentSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    uploaded_by = serializers.CharField(source='uploaded_by.username', read_only=True)
    filename = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    url = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = Attachment
        fields = ['id', 'filename', 'url', 'uploaded_by', 'uploaded_at']

    def get_filename(self, obj):
        return os.path.basename(obj.file.name)

    def get_url(self, obj):
        request = self.context.get('request')
        url = obj.file.url
        return request.build_absolute_uri(url) if request else url


class TicketAttachmentView(APIView):
    """
    GET  /api/tickets/<id>/attachments/   -> list files of a ticket
    POST /api/tickets/<id>/attachments/   -> upload one or MORE files
         (multipart/form-data, field name: "files", repeat it for many files)

    Access: ticket ka owner (customer), assigned support staff, aur Admin.
    """
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]
    parser_classes = [MultiPartParser, FormParser]

    def _get_ticket(self, request, pk):
        ticket = get_object_or_404(Ticket, pk=pk)
        permission = IsTicketParticipant()
        if not permission.has_object_permission(request, self, ticket):
            raise PermissionDenied(permission.message)
        return ticket

    def get(self, request, pk, *args, **kwargs):
        ticket = self._get_ticket(request, pk)
        data = AttachmentSerializer(
            ticket.attachments.select_related('uploaded_by'),
            many=True, context={'request': request},
        ).data
        return Response(data)

    def post(self, request, pk, *args, **kwargs):
        ticket = self._get_ticket(request, pk)

        if ticket.status == Ticket.Status.CLOSED:
            raise ValidationError('Files cannot be added to a closed ticket.')

        # Limits ab admin settings (database) se aati hain
        cfg = SystemSettings.load()
        allowed = {
            '.' + t.strip().lower().lstrip('.')
            for t in cfg.allowed_types_list()
            if t.strip()
        }
        max_files = cfg.max_files_per_request
        max_mb = cfg.max_upload_size_mb

        files = request.FILES.getlist('files')
        if not files:
            raise ValidationError(
                {'files': 'Please upload at least one file (field name: "files").'}
            )
        if len(files) > max_files:
            raise ValidationError(
                {'files': f'You can upload at most {max_files} files at a time.'}
            )

        # Pehle sab validate karo, phir save (ek bhi galat ho to koi save nahi)
        errors = []
        for f in files:
            ext = os.path.splitext(f.name)[1].lower()
            if ext not in allowed:
                errors.append(
                    f"{f.name}: type not allowed. Allowed: "
                    f"{', '.join(sorted(allowed))}"
                )
            elif f.size > max_mb * 1024 * 1024:
                errors.append(f'{f.name}: larger than {max_mb} MB.')
        if errors:
            raise ValidationError({'files': errors})

        created = [
            Attachment.objects.create(
                ticket=ticket, file=f, uploaded_by=request.user
            )
            for f in files
        ]
        data = AttachmentSerializer(
            created, many=True, context={'request': request}
        ).data
        return Response(data, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)


========== accounts/views/change_password_view.py ==========

from django.contrib.auth.password_validation import validate_password
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.authtoken.models import Token
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView


class ChangePasswordView(APIView):
    """
    POST {"old_password": "...", "new_password": "..."}
    Header: Authorization: Token <your_token>
    Changes the password of the logged-in user.
    """
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        old_password = request.data.get('old_password')
        new_password = request.data.get('new_password')

        if not old_password or not new_password:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'old_password and new_password are required.'},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        user = request.user

        if not user.check_password(old_password):
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Old password is incorrect.'},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        if old_password == new_password:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'New password must be different from the old one.'},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        try:
            validate_password(new_password, user)
        except ValidationError as e:
            return Response(
                {'error': list(e.messages)},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        user.set_password(new_password)
        user.save()

        # Old token ko hata ke naya token do (security ke liye)
        Token.objects.filter(user=user).delete()
        new_token = Token.objects.create(user=user)

        return Response(
            {
                'message': 'Password changed successfully.',
                'token': new_token.key,
            },
            status=status.HTTP_200_OK,
        )


========== accounts/views/comment_views.py ==========

from django.core.mail import send_mail
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404
from rest_framework import serializers, status
from rest_framework.exceptions import PermissionDenied, ValidationError
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from accounts.permissions import IsTicketParticipant
from tickets.models import Comment, Ticket


class CommentSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    author = serializers.CharField(source='author.username', read_only=True)
    author_role = serializers.CharField(source='author.role', read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = Comment
        fields = ['id', 'author', 'author_role', 'body', 'is_internal', 'created_at']
        read_only_fields = ['id', 'author', 'author_role', 'created_at']

    def validate_body(self, value):
        value = value.strip()
        if not value:
            raise serializers.ValidationError('Comment cannot be empty.')
        return value


def notify_new_comment(comment):
    """
    Naye (non-internal) comment ka email dusre side ko bhejo.
    Customer likhe -> assigned support ko; staff likhe -> customer ko.
    Development me email server terminal me print hota hai.
    """
    ticket = comment.ticket
    author = comment.author

    if author.pk == ticket.created_by_id:
        target = ticket.assigned_to
    else:
        target = ticket.created_by

    if target and target.email and target.pk != author.pk:
        send_mail(
            subject=f'[Ticket #{ticket.pk}] New comment: {ticket.title}',
            message=f'{author.username} wrote:\n\n{comment.body}',
            from_email=None,
            recipient_list=[target.email],
            fail_silently=True,
        )


class TicketCommentView(APIView):
    """
    GET  /api/tickets/<id>/comments/   -> comment thread (mini chat)
    POST /api/tickets/<id>/comments/   -> {"body": "...", "is_internal": false}

    - Ticket ka owner (customer), assigned support staff aur Admin hi use kar sakte hain.
    - Internal notes sirf staff likh aur dekh sakta hai, customer ko nahi dikhte.
    - Closed ticket par naya comment nahi likh sakte.
    """
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def _get_ticket(self, request, pk):
        ticket = get_object_or_404(
            Ticket.objects.select_related('created_by', 'assigned_to'), pk=pk
        )
        permission = IsTicketParticipant()
        if not permission.has_object_permission(request, self, ticket):
            raise PermissionDenied(permission.message)
        return ticket

    def get(self, request, pk, *args, **kwargs):
        ticket = self._get_ticket(request, pk)
        comments = ticket.comments.select_related('author')
        if request.user.is_customer:
            comments = comments.filter(is_internal=False)
        return Response(CommentSerializer(comments, many=True).data)

    def post(self, request, pk, *args, **kwargs):
        ticket = self._get_ticket(request, pk)

        if ticket.status == Ticket.Status.CLOSED:
            raise ValidationError('Comments cannot be added to a closed ticket.')

        serializer = CommentSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        if serializer.validated_data.get('is_internal') and request.user.is_customer:
            raise PermissionDenied('Customers cannot add internal notes.')

        comment = serializer.save(ticket=ticket, author=request.user)

        if not comment.is_internal:
            notify_new_comment(comment)

        return Response(CommentSerializer(comment).data, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)


========== accounts/views/dashboard_views.py ==========

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.db.models import Count, Q
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from accounts.permissions import IsAdmin, IsCustomer, IsSupportStaff
from tickets.models import Category, Priority, Ticket

User = get_user_model()

OPEN_STATUSES = [Ticket.Status.NEW, Ticket.Status.IN_PROGRESS]


# ------------------------------------------------------------ helpers
def _counts(qs, field):
    """{field_value: number_of_tickets}"""
    rows = qs.values(field).annotate(n=Count('id')).order_by()
    return {row[field]: row['n'] for row in rows}


def by_status(qs):
    counts = _counts(qs, 'status')
    return [
        {'status': value, 'label': label, 'count': counts.get(value, 0)}
        for value, label in Ticket.Status.choices
    ]


def by_priority(qs):
    counts = _counts(qs, 'priority')
    rows = []
    for p in Priority.objects.all():
        count = counts.pop(p.slug, 0)
        if p.is_active or count:
            rows.append({'priority': p.slug, 'name': p.name, 'count': count})
    for slug, count in counts.items():  # purani/anjaan priority (safety)
        rows.append({'priority': slug, 'name': slug, 'count': count})
    return rows


def by_category(qs):
    counts = _counts(qs, 'category_id')
    rows = [
        {'category_id': c.id, 'category': c.name, 'count': counts.pop(c.id, 0)}
        for c in Category.objects.all()
    ]
    uncategorized = counts.pop(None, 0)
    if uncategorized:
        rows.append(
            {'category_id': None, 'category': 'Uncategorized', 'count': uncategorized}
        )
    return rows


def recent(qs, limit=5):
    return list(
        qs.order_by('-created_at').values(
            'id', 'title', 'status', 'priority', 'created_at'
        )[:limit]
    )


# ----------------------------------------------------------- builders
def build_admin_dashboard():
    tickets = Ticket.objects.all()

    # Staff performance: har support staff ke tickets
    staff = (
        User.objects.filter(role='support', is_active=True)
        .annotate(
            assigned_total=Count('assigned_tickets'),
            open_tickets=Count(
                'assigned_tickets',
                filter=Q(assigned_tickets__status__in=OPEN_STATUSES),
            ),
            resolved_tickets=Count(
                'assigned_tickets',
                filter=Q(assigned_tickets__status=Ticket.Status.RESOLVED),
            ),
            closed_tickets=Count(
                'assigned_tickets',
                filter=Q(assigned_tickets__status=Ticket.Status.CLOSED),
            ),
        )
        .order_by('-closed_tickets', '-resolved_tickets', 'id')
    )

    # Average resolution time (hours) per staff
    durations = {}
    resolved_rows = Ticket.objects.filter(
        assigned_to__isnull=False, resolved_at__isnull=False
    ).values_list('assigned_to_id', 'created_at', 'resolved_at')
    for staff_id, created, resolved in resolved_rows:
        durations.setdefault(staff_id, []).append(
            (resolved - created).total_seconds()
        )

    staff_performance = []
    for s in staff:
        secs = durations.get(s.id, [])
        avg_hours = round(sum(secs) / len(secs) / 3600, 2) if secs else None
        staff_performance.append({
            'staff_id': s.id,
            'username': s.username,
            'assigned_total': s.assigned_total,
            'open': s.open_tickets,
            'resolved': s.resolved_tickets,
            'closed': s.closed_tickets,
            'avg_resolution_hours': avg_hours,
        })

    return {
        'role': 'admin',
        'total_tickets': tickets.count(),
        'open_tickets': tickets.filter(status__in=OPEN_STATUSES).count(),
        'unassigned_tickets': tickets.filter(assigned_to__isnull=True)
        .exclude(status=Ticket.Status.CLOSED).count(),
        'by_status': by_status(tickets),
        'by_priority': by_priority(tickets),
        'by_category': by_category(tickets),
        'staff_performance': staff_performance,
    }


def build_support_dashboard(user):
    tickets = Ticket.objects.filter(assigned_to=user)
    base = '/api/support/tickets/'

    # Quick filters: label + kitne tickets + seedha URL
    quick_filters = [
        {'label': row['label'], 'count': row['count'],
         'url': f"{base}?status={row['status']}"}
        for row in by_status(tickets)
    ]
    quick_filters += [
        {'label': f"Priority: {row['name']}", 'count': row['count'],
         'url': f"{base}?priority={row['priority']}"}
        for row in by_priority(tickets) if row['count']
    ]
    quick_filters += [
        {'label': f"Category: {row['category']}", 'count': row['count'],
         'url': f"{base}?category={row['category_id']}"}
        for row in by_category(tickets)
        if row['count'] and row['category_id'] is not None
    ]

    return {
        'role': 'support',
        'assigned_total': tickets.count(),
        'open_tickets': tickets.filter(status__in=OPEN_STATUSES).count(),
        'by_status': by_status(tickets),
        'by_priority': by_priority(tickets),
        'by_category': by_category(tickets),
        'quick_filters': quick_filters,
        'recent_tickets': recent(tickets),
    }


def build_customer_dashboard(user):
    tickets = Ticket.objects.filter(created_by=user)
    return {
        'role': 'customer',
        'total_tickets': tickets.count(),
        'open_tickets': tickets.filter(status__in=OPEN_STATUSES).count(),
        'by_status': by_status(tickets),
        'recent_tickets': recent(tickets, limit=10),
    }


# -------------------------------------------------------------- views
class AdminDashboardView(APIView):
    """GET /api/dashboard/admin/  (Admin only)"""
    permission_classes = [IsAdmin]

    def get(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        return Response(build_admin_dashboard())


class SupportDashboardView(APIView):
    """GET /api/dashboard/support/  (Support Staff only)"""
    permission_classes = [IsSupportStaff]

    def get(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        return Response(build_support_dashboard(request.user))


class CustomerDashboardView(APIView):
    """GET /api/dashboard/me/  (Customer only)"""
    permission_classes = [IsCustomer]

    def get(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        return Response(build_customer_dashboard(request.user))


class DashboardView(APIView):
    """GET /api/dashboard/  -> logged-in user ke role ke hisaab se sahi dashboard"""
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        user = request.user
        if user.is_helpdesk_admin:
            return Response(build_admin_dashboard())
        if user.is_support_staff:
            return Response(build_support_dashboard(user))
        return Response(build_customer_dashboard(user))


========== accounts/views/forgot_reset_view.py ==========

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.contrib.auth.password_validation import validate_password
from django.contrib.auth.tokens import default_token_generator
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.core.mail import send_mail
from django.utils.encoding import force_bytes, force_str
from django.utils.http import urlsafe_base64_decode, urlsafe_base64_encode
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.permissions import AllowAny
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

User = get_user_model()


class ForgotPasswordView(APIView):
    """
    POST {"email": "user@example.com"}
    Sends a password reset uid + token to the user's email.
    (In development, the email is printed in the terminal.)
    """
    permission_classes = [AllowAny]

    def post(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        email = request.data.get('email')
        if not email:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Please provide your email.'},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        user = User.objects.filter(email__iexact=email, is_active=True).first()
        if user:
            uid = urlsafe_base64_encode(force_bytes(user.pk))
            token = default_token_generator.make_token(user)
            send_mail(
                subject='Helpdesk - Password Reset',
                message=(
                    f'Hello {user.username},\n\n'
                    f'Use these details to reset your password:\n'
                    f'uid: {uid}\n'
                    f'token: {token}\n\n'
                    f'If you did not request this, ignore this email.'
                ),
                from_email=None,  # uses DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL
                recipient_list=[user.email],
            )

        # Same response either way, so nobody can find out which emails exist.
        return Response(
            {'message': 'If this email is registered, a reset link has been sent.'},
            status=status.HTTP_200_OK,
        )


class ResetPasswordView(APIView):
    """
    POST {"uid": "...", "token": "...", "new_password": "..."}
    Sets a new password if uid + token are valid.
    """
    permission_classes = [AllowAny]

    def post(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        uid = request.data.get('uid')
        token = request.data.get('token')
        new_password = request.data.get('new_password')

        if not uid or not token or not new_password:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'uid, token and new_password are required.'},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        try:
            user_id = force_str(urlsafe_base64_decode(uid))
            user = User.objects.get(pk=user_id)
        except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError, User.DoesNotExist):
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Invalid reset link.'},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        if not default_token_generator.check_token(user, token):    
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Token is invalid or has expired.'},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        try:
            validate_password(new_password, user)
        except ValidationError as e:
            return Response(
                {'error': list(e.messages)},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        user.set_password(new_password)
        user.save()
        return Response(
            {'message': 'Password reset successful. You can log in now.'},
            status=status.HTTP_200_OK,
        )


========== accounts/views/login_view.py ==========

from django.contrib.auth import authenticate
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.authtoken.models import Token
from rest_framework.permissions import AllowAny
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView


class LoginView(APIView):

    
    """API endpoint for user login.
    Accepts 'username' and 'password', authenticates user,
    and returns an auth token.
    """
    permission_classes = [AllowAny]

    def get(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        return Response(
            {'message': 'Send a POST request with "username" and "password" to log in.'},
            status=status.HTTP_200_OK
        )

    def post(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        username = request.data.get('username')
        password = request.data.get('password')

        if not username or not password:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Please provide both username and password.'},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
            )

        user = authenticate(username=username, password=password)

        if not user:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Invalid username or password.'},
                status=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED
            )

        if not user.is_active:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'User account is disabled.'},
                status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN
            )

        token, _ = Token.objects.get_or_create(user=user)

        return Response(
            {
                'message': 'Login successful.',
                'token': token.key,
                'user': {
                    'id': user.id,
                    'username': user.username,
                    'email': user.email,
                }
            },
            status=status.HTTP_200_OK
        )



========== accounts/views/logout_view.py ==========

from django.contrib.auth import logout
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView


class LogoutView(APIView):
    """
    API endpoint for user logout.
    Requires Token authentication. Deletes the active auth token and clears the session.
    """
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        return Response(
            {'message': 'Send a POST request with Authorization header ("Token <your_token>") to log out.'},
            status=status.HTTP_200_OK
        )

    def post(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        # Delete user's auth token
        if hasattr(request.user, 'auth_token'):
            request.user.auth_token.delete()

        # Flush any session cookies if present
        logout(request)

        return Response(
            {'message': 'Successfully logged out.'},
            status=status.HTTP_200_OK
        )



========== accounts/views/my_tickets_view.py ==========

from rest_framework import generics, serializers
from rest_framework.exceptions import PermissionDenied
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated

from tickets.models import Comment, Ticket


class MyCommentSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    author = serializers.CharField(source='author.username', read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = Comment
        fields = ['id', 'author', 'body', 'created_at']


class MyTicketSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    created_by = serializers.CharField(source='created_by.username', read_only=True)
    assigned_to = serializers.CharField(
        source='assigned_to.username', read_only=True, default=None
    )
    category_name = serializers.CharField(
        source='category.name', read_only=True, default=None
    )
    # tags: ticket banate waqt tag ids bhejo (optional), response mein naam bhi milte hain
    tag_names = serializers.SlugRelatedField(
        source='tags', many=True, read_only=True, slug_field='name'
    )
    comments = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = Ticket
        fields = [
            'id', 'title', 'description', 'category', 'category_name',
            'priority', 'tags', 'tag_names', 'status', 'created_by',
            'assigned_to', 'created_at', 'updated_at', 'resolved_at',
            'comments',
        ]
        read_only_fields = [
            'status', 'created_by', 'assigned_to',
            'created_at', 'updated_at', 'resolved_at',
        ]

    def get_comments(self, obj):
        # Customer ko internal notes nahi dikhane
        visible = obj.comments.filter(is_internal=False)
        return MyCommentSerializer(visible, many=True).data


class MyTicketsView(generics.ListCreateAPIView):
    """
    GET  /api/my-tickets/   -> list of my tickets
                               (filters: ?status=new&priority=high&search=text)
    POST /api/my-tickets/   -> create a new ticket (optional: "tags": [1, 2])
    Header: Authorization: Token <your_token>
    """
    serializer_class = MyTicketSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def get_queryset(self):
        qs = Ticket.objects.filter(created_by=self.request.user)

        status_param = self.request.query_params.get('status')
        priority_param = self.request.query_params.get('priority')
        search = self.request.query_params.get('search')

        if status_param:
            qs = qs.filter(status=status_param)
        if priority_param:
            qs = qs.filter(priority=priority_param)
        if search:
            qs = qs.filter(title__icontains=search)

        return qs.select_related('category', 'assigned_to').prefetch_related('tags')

    def perform_create(self, serializer):
        serializer.save(created_by=self.request.user)


class MyTicketDetailView(generics.RetrieveUpdateAPIView):
    """
    GET   /api/my-tickets/<id>/   -> ticket details with comments
    PATCH /api/my-tickets/<id>/   -> edit title/description/priority/category/tags
                                     (only while status is 'new')
    """
    serializer_class = MyTicketSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]
    lookup_field = 'pk'
    lookup_url_kwarg = 'ticket_id'   # urls.py mein route <int:ticket_id> hai

    def get_queryset(self):
        # Sirf apne tickets, dusre ke ticket pe 404 milega
        return Ticket.objects.filter(
            created_by=self.request.user
        ).prefetch_related('tags')

    def perform_update(self, serializer):
        if serializer.instance.status != Ticket.Status.NEW:
            raise PermissionDenied(
                'You can edit a ticket only while its status is "New".'
            )
        serializer.save()


========== accounts/views/priority_views.py ==========

from rest_framework import generics, serializers
from rest_framework.exceptions import ValidationError

from accounts.views.admin_views import IsAdminOrReadOnly
from tickets.models import Priority, Ticket


class PrioritySerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = Priority
        fields = ['id', 'name', 'slug', 'level', 'is_active']
        read_only_fields = ['id', 'slug']


class PriorityListCreateView(generics.ListCreateAPIView):
    """
    GET  /api/priorities/   -> sab logged-in users (dropdown ke liye).
                               Admin ko inactive bhi dikhte hain.
    POST /api/priorities/   -> Admin: {"name": "Blocker", "level": 5}
    """
    serializer_class = PrioritySerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAdminOrReadOnly]

    def get_queryset(self):
        qs = Priority.objects.all()
        if not self.request.user.is_helpdesk_admin:
            qs = qs.filter(is_active=True)
        return qs


class PriorityDetailView(generics.RetrieveUpdateDestroyAPIView):
    """
    GET    /api/priorities/<id>/   -> sab logged-in users
    PATCH  /api/priorities/<id>/   -> Admin: name, level, is_active badalna
    DELETE /api/priorities/<id>/   -> Admin (agar kisi ticket me use ho raha ho to nahi)
    """
    serializer_class = PrioritySerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAdminOrReadOnly]
    http_method_names = ['get', 'patch', 'delete', 'head', 'options']

    def get_queryset(self):
        qs = Priority.objects.all()
        if not self.request.user.is_helpdesk_admin:
            qs = qs.filter(is_active=True)
        return qs

    def perform_destroy(self, instance):
        if Ticket.objects.filter(priority=instance.slug).exists():
            raise ValidationError(
                'This priority is used by existing tickets. '
                'Set is_active to false instead of deleting it.'
            )
        instance.delete()


========== accounts/views/register_view.py ==========

from tickets.site_settings import SystemSettings
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model, password_validation
from rest_framework import serializers, status
from rest_framework.authtoken.models import Token
from rest_framework.permissions import AllowAny
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

User = get_user_model()


class RegisterSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    password = serializers.CharField(write_only=True)
    password2 = serializers.CharField(write_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = User
        fields = [
            'username', 'email', 'first_name', 'last_name',
            'password', 'password2',
        ]
        extra_kwargs = {'email': {'required': True}}

    def validate_email(self, value):
        if User.objects.filter(email__iexact=value).exists():
            raise serializers.ValidationError('This email is already registered.')
        return value

    def validate(self, attrs):
        if attrs['password'] != attrs['password2']:
            raise serializers.ValidationError(
                {'password2': 'Passwords do not match.'}
            )
        candidate = User(username=attrs.get('username'), email=attrs.get('email'))
        password_validation.validate_password(attrs['password'], candidate)
        return attrs

    def create(self, validated_data):
        validated_data.pop('password2')
        password = validated_data.pop('password')
        # Public registration se hamesha Customer banta hai.
        # Role koi bhi request se nahi le sakta (security).
        return User.objects.create_user(
            password=password, role=User.Role.CUSTOMER, **validated_data
        )


class RegisterView(APIView):
    """
    POST /api/register/
    {"username": "...", "email": "...", "password": "...", "password2": "..."}

    Naya user hamesha 'customer' role ke saath banta hai.
    settings.py me ALLOW_PUBLIC_REGISTRATION = False karne par ye band ho jata hai,
    aur sirf Admin hi /api/admin/users/ se accounts bana sakta hai.
    """
    permission_classes = [AllowAny]

    def post(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        if not SystemSettings.load().allow_public_registration:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Public registration is disabled. '
                          'Please ask an admin to create your account.'},
                status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            )

        serializer = RegisterSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        user = serializer.save()
        token, _ = Token.objects.get_or_create(user=user)

        return Response(
            {
                'message': 'Registration successful.',
                'token': token.key,
                'user': {
                    'id': user.id,
                    'username': user.username,
                    'email': user.email,
                    'role': user.role,
                },
            },
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )


========== accounts/views/report_views.py ==========

from datetime import timedelta

from django.db.models import Count
from django.db.models.functions import TruncDate
from django.http import HttpResponse
from django.utils import timezone
from django.utils.dateparse import parse_date
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.exceptions import ValidationError
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from accounts.permissions import IsAdmin, IsAdminOrSupport
from accounts.report_builders import csv_bytes, pdf_bytes
from accounts.views.dashboard_views import by_category, by_priority, by_status
from accounts.views.ticket_search_view import TicketSearchView
from tickets.models import Priority, Ticket, TicketHistory

MAX_EXPORT_ROWS = 5000
MAX_SUMMARY_DAYS = 366

EXPORT_HEADERS = [
    'ID', 'Title', 'Status', 'Priority', 'Category',
    'Created By', 'Assigned To', 'Created At', 'Updated At', 'Resolved At',
]
# PDF me kam columns (page me fit hone ke liye) - total 273 mm
PDF_HEADERS = [
    'ID', 'Title', 'Status', 'Priority', 'Category',
    'Created By', 'Assigned To', 'Created At',
]
PDF_COL_WIDTHS_MM = [12, 90, 24, 22, 32, 30, 30, 33]


def _fmt(dt):
    return timezone.localtime(dt).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M') if dt else ''


def _filename(extension):
    stamp = timezone.localtime().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M')
    return f'tickets_report_{stamp}.{extension}'


def _export_tickets(request):
    """
    /api/tickets/ jaisa hi queryset (wahi search + filters, wahi role scope),
    bas bina pagination ke, max MAX_EXPORT_ROWS tak.
    """
    view = TicketSearchView()
    view.request = request
    view.args = ()
    view.kwargs = {}
    return list(view.get_queryset()[:MAX_EXPORT_ROWS])


def _priority_names():
    return dict(Priority.objects.values_list('slug', 'name'))


def _rows(tickets, full=True):
    names = _priority_names()
    for t in tickets:
        row = [
            t.id,
            t.title,
            t.get_status_display(),
            names.get(t.priority, t.priority),
            t.category.name if t.category else '',
            t.created_by.username,
            t.assigned_to.username if t.assigned_to else '',
            _fmt(t.created_at),
        ]
        if full:
            row += [_fmt(t.updated_at), _fmt(t.resolved_at)]
        yield row


class TicketsCSVExportView(APIView):
    """
    GET /api/reports/tickets/csv/?status=resolved&created_from=2026-10-01 ...
    Wahi filters jo /api/tickets/ me hain. Admin aur Support Staff ke liye.
    (Admin: sab tickets, Support: sirf apni assigned tickets.)
    """
    permission_classes = [IsAdminOrSupport]

    def get(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        tickets = _export_tickets(request)
        content = csv_bytes(EXPORT_HEADERS, _rows(tickets, full=True))
        response = HttpResponse(content, content_type='text/csv; charset=utf-8')
        response['Content-Disposition'] = f'attachment; filename="{_filename("csv")}"'
        return response


class TicketsPDFExportView(APIView):
    """GET /api/reports/tickets/pdf/?...   (same filters as CSV export)"""
    permission_classes = [IsAdminOrSupport]

    def get(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        tickets = _export_tickets(request)
        filters = request.query_params.urlencode() or 'none'
        subtitle = (
            f'Generated: {_fmt(timezone.now())}  |  '
            f'Tickets: {len(tickets)}  |  Filters: {filters}'
        )
        try:
            content = pdf_bytes(
                'Helpdesk Tickets Report', subtitle,
                PDF_HEADERS, _rows(tickets, full=False), PDF_COL_WIDTHS_MM,
            )
        except ImportError:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'PDF export needs the reportlab package. '
                          'Run: pip install reportlab'},
                status=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            )
        response = HttpResponse(content, content_type='application/pdf')
        response['Content-Disposition'] = f'attachment; filename="{_filename("pdf")}"'
        return response


def _date_param(params, name):
    value = params.get(name)
    if not value:
        return None
    parsed = parse_date(value)
    if parsed is None:
        raise ValidationError({name: 'Use the date format YYYY-MM-DD.'})
    return parsed


class SummaryReportView(APIView):
    """
    GET /api/reports/summary/?from=2026-10-01&to=2026-10-14   (Admin only)
    Default: aakhri 30 din.

    opened   = us period me bani tickets
    resolved = us period me Resolved hui tickets
    closed   = us period me Closed hui tickets (status history se)
    daily    = din-ba-din numbers (chart ke liye)
    """
    permission_classes = [IsAdmin]

    def get(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        params = request.query_params
        date_to = _date_param(params, 'to') or timezone.localdate()
        date_from = _date_param(params, 'from') or (date_to - timedelta(days=29))

        if date_from > date_to:
            raise ValidationError({'from': "'from' cannot be after 'to'."})
        if (date_to - date_from).days + 1 > MAX_SUMMARY_DAYS:
            raise ValidationError(
                {'from': f'Period can be at most {MAX_SUMMARY_DAYS} days.'}
            )

        opened_qs = Ticket.objects.filter(
            created_at__date__gte=date_from, created_at__date__lte=date_to
        )
        resolved_qs = Ticket.objects.filter(
            resolved_at__date__gte=date_from, resolved_at__date__lte=date_to
        )
        closed_events = TicketHistory.objects.filter(
            field='status', new_value=Ticket.Status.CLOSED,
            created_at__date__gte=date_from, created_at__date__lte=date_to,
        )

        # Din-ba-din counts
        def per_day(qs, field):
            rows = (
                qs.annotate(day=TruncDate(field)).values('day')
                .annotate(n=Count('id')).order_by()
            )
            return {r['day']: r['n'] for r in rows}

        opened_by_day = per_day(opened_qs, 'created_at')
        resolved_by_day = per_day(resolved_qs, 'resolved_at')
        closed_by_day = per_day(closed_events, 'created_at')

        daily = []
        day = date_from
        while day <= date_to:
            daily.append({
                'date': day.isoformat(),
                'opened': opened_by_day.get(day, 0),
                'resolved': resolved_by_day.get(day, 0),
                'closed': closed_by_day.get(day, 0),
            })
            day += timedelta(days=1)

        # Average resolution time (hours) us period me resolve hui tickets ka
        seconds = [
            (resolved - created).total_seconds()
            for created, resolved in resolved_qs.values_list('created_at', 'resolved_at')
        ]
        avg_hours = round(sum(seconds) / len(seconds) / 3600, 2) if seconds else None

        return Response({
            'period': {'from': date_from.isoformat(), 'to': date_to.isoformat()},
            'opened': opened_qs.count(),
            'resolved': resolved_qs.count(),
            'closed': closed_events.values('ticket_id').distinct().count(),
            'avg_resolution_hours': avg_hours,
            'opened_by_status': by_status(opened_qs),
            'opened_by_priority': by_priority(opened_qs),
            'opened_by_category': by_category(opened_qs),
            'daily': daily,
        })

========== accounts/views/support_views.py ==========

from django.db.models import Q
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import generics, serializers

from accounts.permissions import IsSupportStaff
from tickets.models import Ticket, TicketHistory

# Ticket lifecycle: New -> In Progress -> Resolved -> Closed
# (Resolved ticket ko wapas In Progress me la sakte hain, reopen ke liye)
ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS = {
    Ticket.Status.NEW: {Ticket.Status.IN_PROGRESS},
    Ticket.Status.IN_PROGRESS: {Ticket.Status.RESOLVED},
    Ticket.Status.RESOLVED: {Ticket.Status.CLOSED, Ticket.Status.IN_PROGRESS},
    Ticket.Status.CLOSED: set(),
}


class SupportTicketSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    created_by = serializers.CharField(source='created_by.username', read_only=True)
    assigned_to = serializers.CharField(
        source='assigned_to.username', read_only=True, default=None
    )
    category_name = serializers.CharField(
        source='category.name', read_only=True, default=None
    )

    class Meta:
        model = Ticket
        fields = [
            'id', 'title', 'description', 'category', 'category_name',
            'priority', 'status', 'created_by', 'assigned_to',
            'created_at', 'updated_at', 'resolved_at',
        ]
        # Support staff sirf status aur priority badal sakta hai
        read_only_fields = [
            'title', 'description', 'category', 'created_by', 'assigned_to',
            'created_at', 'updated_at', 'resolved_at',
        ]

    def validate_status(self, new_status):
        current = self.instance.status
        if new_status == current:
            return new_status
        if new_status not in ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS.get(current, set()):
            raise serializers.ValidationError(
                f"Cannot move a ticket from '{current}' to '{new_status}'."
            )
        return new_status


class SupportTicketListView(generics.ListAPIView):
    """
    GET /api/support/tickets/
    Tickets assigned to the logged-in support staff.
    Filters: ?status=new  ?priority=high  ?category=1  ?search=<id or title>
    """
    serializer_class = SupportTicketSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsSupportStaff]

    def get_queryset(self):
        qs = Ticket.objects.filter(assigned_to=self.request.user)
        params = self.request.query_params

        if params.get('status'):
            qs = qs.filter(status=params['status'])
        if params.get('priority'):
            qs = qs.filter(priority=params['priority'])
        if params.get('category'):
            qs = qs.filter(category_id=params['category'])

        search = params.get('search')
        if search:
            condition = Q(title__icontains=search)
            if search.isdigit():
                condition |= Q(pk=int(search))
            qs = qs.filter(condition)

        return qs.select_related('category', 'created_by', 'assigned_to')


class SupportTicketDetailView(generics.RetrieveUpdateAPIView):
    """
    GET   /api/support/tickets/<id>/   -> ticket details
    PATCH /api/support/tickets/<id>/   -> {"status": "...", "priority": "..."}
    Only tickets assigned to this staff member are visible.
    """
    serializer_class = SupportTicketSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsSupportStaff]
    http_method_names = ['get', 'patch', 'head', 'options']

    def get_queryset(self):
        return Ticket.objects.filter(assigned_to=self.request.user)

    def perform_update(self, serializer):
        ticket = serializer.instance
        old_status, old_priority = ticket.status, ticket.priority
        new_status = serializer.validated_data.get('status', old_status)

        extra = {}
        if new_status == Ticket.Status.RESOLVED and old_status != Ticket.Status.RESOLVED:
            extra['resolved_at'] = timezone.now()
        elif old_status == Ticket.Status.RESOLVED and new_status == Ticket.Status.IN_PROGRESS:
            extra['resolved_at'] = None

        ticket = serializer.save(**extra)

        # History me likho kya badla
        user = self.request.user
        if ticket.status != old_status:
            TicketHistory.objects.create(
                ticket=ticket, changed_by=user, field='status',
                old_value=old_status, new_value=ticket.status,
            )
        if ticket.priority != old_priority:
            TicketHistory.objects.create(
                ticket=ticket, changed_by=user, field='priority',
                old_value=old_priority, new_value=ticket.priority,
            )


========== accounts/views/system_settings_view.py ==========

"""Admin settings API (spec 3.10): sirf admin padh aur badal sakta hai."""
from drf_spectacular.utils import extend_schema
from rest_framework import serializers
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from accounts.permissions import IsAdmin
from tickets.site_settings import SystemSettings


class SystemSettingsSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    max_upload_size_mb = serializers.IntegerField(min_value=1, max_value=50)
    max_files_per_request = serializers.IntegerField(min_value=1, max_value=20)

    class Meta:
        model = SystemSettings
        fields = [
            'allow_public_registration',
            'max_upload_size_mb',
            'max_files_per_request',
            'allowed_file_types',
            'notifications_enabled',
            'updated_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = ['updated_at']

    def validate_allowed_file_types(self, value):
        types = [t.strip().lower().lstrip('.') for t in value.split(',') if t.strip()]
        if not types:
            raise serializers.ValidationError('Kam se kam ek file type do.')
        for t in types:
            if not t.isalnum():
                raise serializers.ValidationError(f'Galat file type: {t}')
        return ','.join(types)


class SystemSettingsView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsAdmin]

    @extend_schema(responses=SystemSettingsSerializer, tags=['Admin settings'])
    def get(self, request):
        data = SystemSettingsSerializer(SystemSettings.load()).data
        return Response(data)

    @extend_schema(
        request=SystemSettingsSerializer,
        responses=SystemSettingsSerializer,
        tags=['Admin settings'],
    )
    def patch(self, request):
        obj = SystemSettings.load()
        serializer = SystemSettingsSerializer(obj, data=request.data, partial=True)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        serializer.save()
        return Response(serializer.data)

========== accounts/views/ticket_search_view.py ==========

from django.db.models import Q
from django.utils.dateparse import parse_date
from rest_framework import generics, serializers
from rest_framework.exceptions import ValidationError
from rest_framework.pagination import PageNumberPagination
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated

from tickets.models import Priority, Ticket

ALLOWED_ORDERING = {
    'id', '-id', 'created_at', '-created_at',
    'updated_at', '-updated_at', 'status', '-status',
}


class TicketPagination(PageNumberPagination):
    page_size = 10
    page_size_query_param = 'page_size'
    max_page_size = 100


class TicketListSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    created_by = serializers.CharField(source='created_by.username', read_only=True)
    assigned_to = serializers.CharField(
        source='assigned_to.username', read_only=True, default=None
    )
    category_name = serializers.CharField(
        source='category.name', read_only=True, default=None
    )
    tag_names = serializers.SlugRelatedField(
        source='tags', many=True, read_only=True, slug_field='name'
    )
    description_preview = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = Ticket
        fields = [
            'id', 'title', 'description_preview', 'status', 'priority',
            'category', 'category_name', 'tag_names', 'created_by',
            'assigned_to', 'created_at', 'updated_at', 'resolved_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = fields

    def get_description_preview(self, obj):
        text = obj.description or ''
        return text if len(text) <= 120 else text[:120] + '...'


def _csv(params, name):
    """'?status=new,in_progress' -> ['new', 'in_progress']"""
    value = params.get(name)
    if not value:
        return []
    return [v.strip() for v in value.split(',') if v.strip()]


def _date(params, name):
    value = params.get(name)
    if not value:
        return None
    parsed = parse_date(value)  # sirf YYYY-MM-DD
    if parsed is None:
        raise ValidationError({name: 'Use the date format YYYY-MM-DD.'})
    return parsed


class TicketSearchView(generics.ListAPIView):
    """
    GET /api/tickets/    (paginated: count, next, previous, results)

    Kaun kya dekhta hai:
      Admin -> sab tickets | Support Staff -> assigned tickets | Customer -> apni tickets

    SEARCH
      ?search=<text>      ticket ID (12 ya #12), title, description ya comment ke keyword.
                          (Customer ke liye internal notes search me nahi aate.)
    FILTERS (comma se multiple values: ?status=new,in_progress)
      ?status=            new / in_progress / resolved / closed
      ?priority=          priority slug (low, medium, high, critical ...)
      ?category=          category id(s), ya 'none' (bina category)
      ?tag=               tag ka naam (bug ya bug,urgent), kisi ek bhi tag wali tickets
      ?assigned_to=       (sirf Admin) user id ya 'unassigned'
      ?created_from=YYYY-MM-DD   &   ?created_to=YYYY-MM-DD   (date range, dono inclusive)
    ORDERING & PAGES
      ?ordering=-created_at (default) | created_at | id | updated_at | status
      ?page=2   ?page_size=20 (max 100)
    """
    serializer_class = TicketListSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]
    pagination_class = TicketPagination

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        params = self.request.query_params
        is_staff_side = user.is_helpdesk_admin or user.is_support_staff

        # 1) Role ke hisaab se base queryset
        if user.is_helpdesk_admin:
            qs = Ticket.objects.all()
        elif user.is_support_staff:
            qs = Ticket.objects.filter(assigned_to=user)
        else:
            qs = Ticket.objects.filter(created_by=user)

        # 2) Search (ID / title / description / comments)
        search = params.get('search', '').strip()
        if search:
            condition = Q(title__icontains=search) | Q(description__icontains=search)
            number = search.lstrip('#')
            if number.isdigit():
                condition |= Q(pk=int(number))
            if is_staff_side:
                condition |= Q(comments__body__icontains=search)
            else:
                condition |= Q(
                    comments__body__icontains=search, comments__is_internal=False
                )
            qs = qs.filter(condition).distinct()

        # 3) Filters
        statuses = _csv(params, 'status')
        if statuses:
            invalid = [s for s in statuses if s not in Ticket.Status.values]
            if invalid:
                raise ValidationError(
                    {'status': f"Invalid status: {', '.join(invalid)}. "
                               f"Use: {', '.join(Ticket.Status.values)}"}
                )
            qs = qs.filter(status__in=statuses)

        priorities = _csv(params, 'priority')
        if priorities:
            known = set(Priority.objects.values_list('slug', flat=True))
            invalid = [p for p in priorities if p not in known]
            if invalid:
                raise ValidationError(
                    {'priority': f"Invalid priority: {', '.join(invalid)}."}
                )
            qs = qs.filter(priority__in=priorities)

        categories = _csv(params, 'category')
        if categories:
            ids = [int(c) for c in categories if c.isdigit()]
            wants_none = 'none' in categories
            if len(ids) + int(wants_none) != len(categories):
                raise ValidationError(
                    {'category': "Use category ids (e.g. 1,2) or 'none'."}
                )
            condition = Q(category_id__in=ids)
            if wants_none:
                condition |= Q(category__isnull=True)
            qs = qs.filter(condition)

        tag_names = _csv(params, 'tag')
        if tag_names:
            tag_condition = Q()
            for name in tag_names:
                tag_condition |= Q(tags__name__iexact=name)
            qs = qs.filter(tag_condition).distinct()

        assigned = params.get('assigned_to')
        if assigned and user.is_helpdesk_admin:
            if assigned == 'unassigned':
                qs = qs.filter(assigned_to__isnull=True)
            elif assigned.isdigit():
                qs = qs.filter(assigned_to_id=int(assigned))
            else:
                raise ValidationError(
                    {'assigned_to': "Use a user id or 'unassigned'."}
                )

        date_from = _date(params, 'created_from')
        date_to = _date(params, 'created_to')
        if date_from and date_to and date_from > date_to:
            raise ValidationError(
                {'created_from': 'created_from cannot be after created_to.'}
            )
        if date_from:
            qs = qs.filter(created_at__date__gte=date_from)
        if date_to:
            qs = qs.filter(created_at__date__lte=date_to)

        # 4) Ordering
        ordering = params.get('ordering', '-created_at')
        if ordering not in ALLOWED_ORDERING:
            raise ValidationError(
                {'ordering': f"Use one of: {', '.join(sorted(ALLOWED_ORDERING))}"}
            )

        return (
            qs.select_related('category', 'created_by', 'assigned_to')
            .prefetch_related('tags')
            .order_by(ordering, '-id')
        )


========== accounts/views/user_profile_view.py ==========

from rest_framework import generics, serializers
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model

User = get_user_model()


class UserProfileSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    total_tickets = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = User
        fields = [
            'id', 'username', 'email', 'first_name', 'last_name',
            'role', 'date_joined', 'total_tickets',
        ]
        # Ye fields user khud change nahi kar sakta
        # (khaas karke role, warna customer khud ko admin bana lega)
        read_only_fields = ['id', 'username', 'role', 'date_joined']

    def get_total_tickets(self, obj):
        return obj.created_tickets.count()

    def validate_email(self, value):
        user = self.instance
        if User.objects.exclude(pk=user.pk).filter(email__iexact=value).exists():
            raise serializers.ValidationError('This email is already in use.')
        return value


class UserProfileView(generics.RetrieveUpdateAPIView):
    """
    GET   /api/profile/   -> logged-in user ki profile
    PATCH /api/profile/   -> email, first_name, last_name update karna
    Header: Authorization: Token <your_token>
    """
    serializer_class = UserProfileSerializer
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def get_object(self):
        return self.request.user

========== config/urls.py ==========

"""
URL configuration for config project.
"""
from django.conf import settings
from django.conf.urls.static import static
from django.contrib import admin
from django.http import JsonResponse
from django.urls import include, path


def api_status(request):
    return JsonResponse({
        'status': 'online',
        'message': 'Helpdesk Ticket System API is running.',
        'endpoints': {
            'admin': '/admin/',
            'login': '/api/login/',
            'logout': '/api/logout/',
            'docs': '/api/docs/',
        }
    })


urlpatterns = [
    # Web UI (login, dashboard, ...). Root '/' login page kholta hai.
    path('', include('accounts.ui_urls')),

    # API status (pehle '/' par tha)
    path('api/status/', api_status, name='api-status'),

    path('admin/', admin.site.urls),
    path('api/', include('accounts.urls')),
    path('api/', include('tickets.urls')),
]

# Development mein uploaded files (attachments) browser mein kholne ke liye
if settings.DEBUG and getattr(settings, 'MEDIA_URL', ''):
    urlpatterns += static(settings.MEDIA_URL, document_root=settings.MEDIA_ROOT)




========== config/wsgi.py ==========

"""
WSGI config for config project.

It exposes the WSGI callable as a module-level variable named ``application``.

For more information on this file, see
https://docs.djangoproject.com/en/6.1/howto/deployment/wsgi/
"""

import os

from django.core.wsgi import get_wsgi_application

os.environ.setdefault('DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE', 'config.settings')

application = get_wsgi_application()



========== manage.py ==========

#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Django's command-line utility for administrative tasks."""
import os
import sys


def main():
    """Run administrative tasks."""
    os.environ.setdefault('DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE', 'config.settings')
    try:
        from django.core.management import execute_from_command_line
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise ImportError(
            "Couldn't import Django. Are you sure it's installed and "
            "available on your PYTHONPATH environment variable? Did you "
            "forget to activate a virtual environment?"
        ) from exc
    execute_from_command_line(sys.argv)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

        Wahi filters jo /api/tickets/ me hain. Admin aur Support Staff ke liye.
        (Admin: sab tickets, Support: sirf apni assigned tickets.)
      summary: Tickets CSV export
      parameters:
      - in: query
        name: assigned_to
        schema:
          type: string
        description: (Sirf Admin) staff user id ya 'unassigned'.
      - in: query
        name: category
        schema:
          type: string
        description: Category id(s) comma se, ya 'none' (bina category).
      - in: query
        name: created_from
        schema:
          type: string
          format: date
        description: YYYY-MM-DD (is din ko mila kar).
      - in: query
        name: created_to
        schema:
          type: string
          format: date
        description: YYYY-MM-DD (is din ko mila kar).
      - in: query
        name: ordering
        schema:
          type: string
          enum:
          - -created_at
          - -id
          - -status
          - -updated_at
          - created_at
          - id
          - status
          - updated_at
        description: 'Default: -created_at.'
      - in: query
        name: priority
        schema:
          type: string
        description: Comma se multiple priority slugs (low, medium, high, critical,
          ...).
      - in: query
        name: search
        schema:
          type: string
        description: 'Ticket ID (12 ya #12), title, description ya comment ka keyword.'
      - in: query
        name: status
        schema:
          type: string
        description: 'Comma se multiple: new, in_progress, resolved, closed.'
      tags:
      - Reports
      security:
      - tokenAuth: []
      - cookieAuth: []
      responses:
        '200':
          content:
            text/csv:
              schema:
                type: string
                format: binary
          description: ''

components:
  schemas:
    AdminTicket:
      type: object
      properties:
        id:
          type: integer
          readOnly: true
        title:
          type: string
          readOnly: true
        priority:
          type: string
          readOnly: true
        status:
          allOf:
          - $ref: '#/components/schemas/StatusEnum'
          readOnly: true
        category:
          type: integer
          readOnly: true
          nullable: true
        category_name:
          type: string
          readOnly: true
        created_by:
          type: string
          readOnly: true
        assigned_to:
          type: string
          readOnly: true
        assigned_to_id:
          type: integer
          readOnly: true
        created_at:
          type: string
          format: date-time
          readOnly: true
        updated_at:
          type: string
          format: date-time
          readOnly: true
        resolved_at:
          type: string
          format: date-time
          readOnly: true
          nullable: true
      required:
      - assigned_to
      - assigned_to_id
      - category
      - category_name
      - created_at
      - created_by
      - id
      - priority
      - resolved_at
      - status
      - title
      - updated_at
    AdminTicketDetail:
      type: object
      properties:
        id:
          type: integer
          readOnly: true
        title:
          type: string
          maxLength: 200
        description:
          type: string
        category:
          type: integer
